Total even issues: 35
======================================================================
ISSUE #290: [P1][ui/interaction] Empty weak-stub constructors of trimmed QGIS widgets are reachable from compiled UI paths - constructing them is UB/crash (Expression Builder/LineEdit, graduated histogram, curve editor, rich text editor, JSON editor)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Empty weak-stub constructors of trimmed QGIS widgets are reachable from compiled UI paths - constructing them is UB/crash (Expression Builder/LineEdit, graduated histogram, curve editor, rich text editor, JSON editor). This issue aggregates 8 related finding(s): UIF-1, UIF-2, UIF-3, UIF-4, UIF-5, VPATCH-1, VPATCH-2, UIF-8. Specifically, Expression Builder dialogs embed QgsExpressionBuilderWidget whose constructor links to an empty weak stub - opening any expression editor constructs an uninitialized QObject (UB/crash). Specifically, QgsExpressionLineEdit embedded in 6 live forms (incl. the Processing feature-source options page) links to the empty weak-stub constructor - constructing those pages is UB/crash. Specifically, Graduated renderer form embeds QgsGraduatedHistogramWidget whose ctor is an empty weak stub - selecting the Graduated renderer crashes the Symbology tab.

## Area
GUI / Application Shell / Dialogs

## Type
ui/interaction

## Severity
P1

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/ui/qgsexpressionbuilderdialogbase.ui:21-28,42-49 (also src/ui/qgsexpressionselectiondialogbase.ui:83, src/ui/qgsfieldcalculatorbase.ui:188-196)` (QgsExpressionBuilderWidget 'builder' / 'mExpressionBuilder')
- `src/ui/processing/qgsprocessingfeaturesourceoptionsbase.ui:95 (plus src/ui/symbollayer/qgsgeometrygeneratorwidgetbase.ui:18, src/ui/qgsdbimportvectorlayerdialog.ui:292, src/ui/attributeformconfig/qgsattributetypeedit.ui:213, src/ui/numericformats/qgsexpressionbasednumericformatwidgetbase.ui:30, src/ui/processing/qgsprocessingvectortilewriterlayerdetailswidgetbase.ui:72)` (mFilterExpressionWidget / modificationExpressionSelector / mExpressionWidget / mExpressionSelector / mEditFilterExpression)
- `src/ui/qgsgraduatedsymbolrendererwidget.ui:496-500,545-549` (mHistogramWidget)
- `src/ui/qgspropertyassistantwidgetbase.ui:197-201` (mCurveEditor)
- `src/ui/annotations/qgsannotationlinetextwidgetbase.ui:72 (also src/ui/annotations/qgsannotationpointtextwidgetbase.ui:132, src/ui/annotations/qgsannotationrectangulartextwidgetbase.ui:226, src/ui/processing/qgsprocessinghelpeditorwidgetbase.ui:137)` (mTextEdit / mRichTextEdit)
- `src/gui/CMakeLists.txt:1912-1987 (trim) + src/gui/antigravity_qgsgui_stubs.c:344-345,527-529 (stubs)` (QgsExpressionLineEdit::QgsExpressionLineEdit / QgsExpressionBuilderWidget::QgsExpressionBuilderWidget)
- `src/gui/antigravity_qgsgui_stubs.c:743-781 (JsonEditWidget), 1271-1293 (SensorThingsSubsetEditor), 791-873 (LayoutHtmlWidget)` (QgsJsonEditWidget::QgsJsonEditWidget / QgsSensorThingsSubsetEditor::QgsSensorThingsSubsetEditor / QgsLayoutHtmlWidget::QgsLayoutHtmlWidget)
- `src/ui/qgsattributeactionpropertiesdialogbase.ui:90-96,266-270` (mActionText)

## User impact
- Every expression-editing entry point in the vendored QGIS shell (data-defined overrides in labeling, layer properties expression insert, field calculator, select-by-expression) is a crash path in the shipped app.
- Processing vector-input options and the geometry-generator symbology workflow (plus DB import filter, attribute-form config expression, numeric-format expression widget) are crash paths in the app.
- One of the three core vector rendering modes is unusable; switching renderers in layer styling crashes the app.
- The whole property-assistant feature (non-linear data-defined size/rotation/transparency) is a crash path.
- Text annotation item creation crashes; processing model help editor (model designer, if reachable) same.
- Hard crash on multiple user-reachable UI paths in the shipped app (attribute table, processing dialogs). Same hazard class was acknowledged in-repo for the raster histogram widget but left unfixed for these widgets.
- Crash or corruption on feature forms with JSON fields; SensorThings/layout-HTML paths are lower-reachability but same mechanism.
- Whole Actions feature degraded to data loss: existing actions destroyed on edit, new actions impossible.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (UIF-1)**: Right-click a vector layer -> Properties (or Ctrl+I) -> Labels tab -> click any data-defined override button -> 'Edit Expression...' (also: Display tab 'Insert Expression' button, attribute table field calculator icon, select-by-expression).
- **Trigger (UIF-2)**: In any Processing algorithm dialog with a vector input, click the input's options (...) button -> '<param> Options' panel opens; or in layer styling add a Geometry Generator symbol layer.
- **Trigger (UIF-3)**: Open vector layer properties, Symbology, choose Graduated renderer.
- **Trigger (UIF-4)**: Open any data-defined property assistant (layer styling/labeling -> override button -> Assistant...).
- **Trigger (UIF-5)**: Create any text annotation item in the annotation layer workflow.
- **Trigger (VPATCH-1)**: Open attribute table on a vector layer, open the sort dialog (or edit a stored filter expression); or open the source options panel of any vector parameter in an algorithm dialog.
- **Trigger (VPATCH-2)**: Open a feature form for a layer containing a JSON-type attribute.
- **Trigger (UIF-8)**: Edit an existing layer action and press OK; or try to create a new action.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (UIF-1)**: The Expression Builder dialog opens with a functional expression tree + editor.
- **Expected (UIF-2)**: Options panel / geometry-generator symbol layer widget opens with an expression line edit.
- **Expected (UIF-3)**: Graduated renderer page with histogram view.
- **Expected (UIF-4)**: Assistant dialog with an interactive curve editor.
- **Expected (UIF-5)**: Item properties widget with rich-text editor.
- **Expected (VPATCH-1)**: Either the implementations are compiled, or every construction site is guarded/removed as was done for QgsRasterHistogramWidget (qgsrasterlayerproperties.cpp:421-423) and QgsHistoryProviderRegistry defaults (qgsgui.cpp:355-356).
- **Expected (VPATCH-2)**: Widget trimmed from the build should not be registered/constructible from compiled code.
- **Expected (UIF-8)**: Dialog shows the current action command; edits are saved.

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (UIF-1)**: uic setupUi allocates QgsExpressionBuilderWidget via operator new and 'constructs' it with the empty stub: QObject/QWidget base constructors never run, vptr and d_ptr are uninitialized garbage; the very next member call (setObjectName in setupUi, then the dialog's initGui access) dereferences garbage - deterministic UB, near-certain segfault.
- **Actual (UIF-2)**: QgsExpressionLineEdit instance is raw uninitialized memory (no QObject base constructed, no vtable); first signal connect or property access is UB - crash or garbage behavior.
- **Actual (UIF-3)**: mHistogramWidget is uninitialized memory; first use (setObjectName during setupUi or the widget's own ctor-time connect at qgsgraduatedsymbolrendererwidget.cpp) hits a garbage vptr - UB/crash.
- **Actual (UIF-4)**: mCurveEditor is an uninitialized QObject; the assistant's ctor-time property access on it is UB - crash.
- **Actual (UIF-5)**: mTextEdit is uninitialized memory; widget setup dereferences a garbage vptr/d_ptr - UB/crash.
- **Actual (VPATCH-1)**: Construction runs a no-op constructor: QWidget/QObject bases are never initialized (d_ptr and vptr are garbage), then layout->addWidget()/virtual calls dereference uninitialized state — undefined behavior, in practice segfault or memory corruption.
- **Actual (VPATCH-2)**: Construction yields an uninitialized QWidget — UB/crash when the form adds or shows the widget (or silently broken JSON editing if it happens not to fault).
- **Actual (UIF-8)**: Command field always blank and untypable (inert widget); pressing OK stores actionText = "" - the action's command is silently wiped; 'Insert Field'/'Insert Expression' buttons are no-ops.

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Tried: (a) does a strong definition override the weak stub? nm -D on build src/gui/libqgis_gui.so shows only W (weak) defs; disassembly of the shipped ctor is `ret` + nops (no base init, no vptr write, not even push/pop); objdump shows dozens of `call <X ctor>@plt` from compiled consumers (QgsDualView, uic setupUi, QgsExpressionBuilderDialog, QgsProcessing*). (b) are real .cpp compiled elsewhere? all REMOVE_ITEM'd from QGIS_GUI_SRCS (src/gui/CMakeLists.txt 1908-1963), no other target compiles them. (c) is setupUi executable? yes - uic calls setObjectName() immediately on the garbage object -> d_ptr deref -> UB/crash. (d) reachability: layer properties (active_view_host.cpp:419; triggered from app menus main_window_layers.cpp:251/297, layer_tree_menu.cpp:78, main_window_connections.cpp:420), attribute table (main_window_menus.cpp:203->openAttributeTable), processing options - all first-party compiled paths confirmed. Could not refute; P1 holds for UIF-1/2/3/VPATCH-1 (user-visible crash), lower for the rest.
- **Review note**: No line drift (CMakeLists trim block 1908-1963; stubs at antigravity_qgsgui_stubs.c:280-281/344-345/528-529/688-689/744-745/792-793/1088-1089/1272-1273). Severity corrections: UIF-4 & VPATCH-2 downgraded to P2, UIF-5 to P3 (low app reachability). Structural note: members UIF-1..5/VPATCH-1/2 are one homogeneous machine-level class (weak-stub ctor of a REMOVE_ITEM'd widget still constructible from compiled callers); VPATCH-1 duplicates UIF-1/UIF-2 sites and should be merged into them rather than kept separate.
- **Finding UIF-1**: `Form: <widget class="QgsExpressionBuilderWidget" name="builder" native="true"> with customwidget header qgsexpressionbuilderwidget.h. ## Suggested fix
- Ensure widgets are fully initialized or stubbed safely, signals/slots are correctly connected to backend results, and UI elements do not block the main event loop.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (UIF-1, UIF-2, UIF-3, UIF-4, UIF-5, VPATCH-1, VPATCH-2, UIF-8).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

======================================================================
ISSUE #292: [P1][error-handling] Predict-only classification silently skips feature scaling when sidecar missing/corrupt (#242 fix incomplete: ModelSidecarMissing never set)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Predict-only classification silently skips feature scaling when sidecar missing/corrupt (#242 fix incomplete: ModelSidecarMissing never set). Specifically, Predict-only classification silently skips feature scaling when model sidecar .meta.json is missing or corrupt (#242 fix incomplete: ModelSidecarMissing is never set).

## Area
Error Handling / Exception Safety

## Type
error-handling

## Severity
P1

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/analysis/classification/rs_classification_pipeline.cpp:194-254 (silent ignore), 797-806 (scaler skipped when unfitted); enum at rs_classification_pipeline.h:72` (RsClassificationPipeline::run)

## User impact
- Silent wrong scientific output in the default scale=true configuration; the P1 defect #242 was declared fixed (commit 880e98f138) but the guard was never wired.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (RSCLS-1)**: Train with scale=true (operator default), then run rs:supervised_classification with modelIn pointing at the .yml after the .meta.json was not copied/corrupted (e.g. user shares only the model file).

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (RSCLS-1)**: Run fails with Error::ModelSidecarMissing / operator raises FileNotReadable so the user knows the scaler is unavailable (that was the stated fix for #242).

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (RSCLS-1)**: Scaler stays unfitted; tile loop's config.scaler.isFitted() is false so raw DN features are fed to a model trained on standardized features; run returns ok=true and writes a confidently wrong class map.

## Root cause
- **RSCLS-1**: CONFIRMED: full-repo grep shows Error::ModelSidecarMissing defined only in rs_classification_pipeline.h:72 with ZERO setting sites in any src/ or tests/ file. rs_classification_pipeline.cpp:194-202 predict-only sidecar block has no else branch: loadModelSidecar returns false for missing file / non-object JSON / wrong version (:113-130) and run() silently proceeds with unfitted config.scaler; tile loop guards on config.scaler.isFitted() (:794-800) so raw DN are fed to a model trained on standardized features; run returns ok=true and writes a wrong class map. GUI loadClassifierModel (qgsclassificationmainwindow.cpp:3304-3367) only logs + shows a 6s statusbar warning for a corrupt sidecar and nothing for a missing one, then proceeds. Fix commit 880e98f138 (fixes #242) added ONLY the enum (plus a seed param) - git show confirmed - so #242's guard was never wired.
  - *Callchain*: `rs:supervised_classification operator (modelIn set) -> RsClassificationPipeline::run; GUI QgsClassificationMainWindow::loadClassifierModel warns in status bar only and applyClassification proceeds with unfitted m_loadedScaler`
  - *Code context*: `if ( loadModelSidecar( config.modelLoadPath, ... ) ) { if ( sidecarScaler.isFitted() ) config.scaler = sidecarScaler; ... } — no else branch; the Error::ModelSidecarMissing enum value added by fix commit 880e98f138 has zero references in any .cpp (grep over src/ and tests/).`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Tried: (a) maybe another place sets scaler/error for predict-only: checked rs_supervised_classification_operator and QgsClassificationMainWindow apply path (cfg.scaler = m_loadedScaler; m_loadedScaler reset to unfitted default when sidecar missing/corrupt) - no error surfaced. (b) maybe the sidecar is unconditionally written next to the model so missing/corrupt can't happen in practice: confirmed train path writes it, but copy-only-model / corrupted version-1 file is the stated trigger and loadModelSidecar's early-false paths are real. (c) commit 880e98f138 diff reviewed: +ModelSidecarMissing enum and +seed; no set-site.
- **Review note**: Line refs exact (194-254/797-806; enum at h:72). Severity kept P1: silent wrong scientific output in the default scale=true configuration when the sidecar is absent/corrupt, matching the P1 severity of the original #242. GUI path has a mild non-blocking warning, headless/operator path is fully silent.
- **Finding RSCLS-1**: `if ( loadModelSidecar( config.modelLoadPath, ... ) ) { if ( sidecarScaler.isFitted() ) config.scaler = sidecarScaler; ... } — no else branch; the Error::ModelSidecarMissing enum value added by fix commit 880e98f138 has zero references in any .cpp (grep over src/ and tests/).`

## Suggested fix
- Implement robust error reporting, return explicit error codes/exceptions, and prevent silent failures or unhandled exception aborts.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (RSCLS-1).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

ISSUE #294: [P2][ui/interaction] Stubbed code-editor widgets silently return empty text: OK/Apply wipes layer provider subsets, map tips, actions, labeling expressions
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Stubbed code-editor widgets silently return empty text: OK/Apply wipes layer provider subsets, map tips, actions, labeling expressions. This issue aggregates 4 related finding(s): UIF-6, UIF-7, UIF-9, UIF-10. Specifically, Vector layer properties Map Tips editor is a stubbed QgsCodeEditorHTML - template never displays, cannot accept typing, and OK wipes the layer's mapTipTemplate. Specifically, Vector layer Source-tab subset string and the Query Builder dialog wipe the layer's provider filter - stubbed QgsCodeEditorSQL text() always returns empty. Specifically, Point-cloud layer properties subset-string editor is a stubbed QgsCodeEditorSQL - filter never displayed and wiped on apply.

## Area
GUI / Application Shell / Dialogs

## Type
ui/interaction

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

- `src/ui/qgspointcloudlayerpropertiesbase.ui:357,659` (txtSubsetSQL)
- `src/ui/qgstextformatwidgetbase.ui:5720-5726,7148-7152` (mGeometryGenerator)

- Silent data loss of layer-level configuration on a routine OK click, plus a dead Map Tips editing UI.

- Same silent-filter-wipe class as UIF-7 at lower reachability (point-cloud layers in this RS app).

- **Actual (UIF-9)**: Filter blank, wiped to "" on apply.

ISSUE #296: [P2][processing/provider] 13 of 30 handcrafted OTB wrappers can never execute or use wrong parameter grammar (nonexistent apps, invented keys, extent swap)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
13 of 30 handcrafted OTB wrappers can never execute or use wrong parameter grammar (nonexistent apps, invented keys, extent swap). This issue aggregates 2 related finding(s): PROVO-1, PROVO-4. Specifically, 13 of 30 handcrafted OTB wrappers can never execute: 4 target nonexistent OTB applications and 9 pass invented parameter keys (#172 fixed only 6 apps). Specifically, otb_extract_roi maps QGIS extent components to swapped lrx/lry and omits -mode.extent.unit, so extent-mode ROI extraction uses the wrong window.

## Area
Processing Framework / GDAL & OTB Providers

## Type
processing/provider

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/processing/providers/otb_tools/algorithms/:otb_lsms.h:16; otb_feature_extraction.h:16; otb_gray_level_cooccurrence_matrix.h:16; otb_pixel_info.h:16; otb_segmentation.cpp:62-118; otb_concatenate_images.cpp:21; otb_compute_images_statistics.cpp:50; otb_haralick_texture.cpp:24; otb_binary_morphological.cpp:29-33; otb_gray_scale_morphological.cpp:31-35; otb_multi_resolution_pyramid.cpp:32-36; otb_bundle_to_perfect_sensor.cpp:22-23; otb_radiometric_indices.cpp:23-24` (applicationName()/buildArgs() of 13 OtbToolWrapper subclasses)
- `src/processing/providers/otb_tools/algorithms/otb_extract_roi.cpp:24-33` (OtbExtractRoiAlgorithm::buildArgs)

## User impact
- 13/30 (43%) of the handcrafted OTB tools advertised in the Processing toolbox are dead weight; users get obscure OTB CLI errors or 'application not found' for core RS operations (segmentation, texture, morphology, pan-sharpening, statistics)
- ROI/crop tool unusable; may also throw OTB errors when the crossed rectangle is empty

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (PROVO-1)**: Run any of: otb_lsms, otb_feature_extraction, otb_gray_level_cooccurrence_matrix, otb_pixel_info, otb_segmentation, otb_concatenate_images, otb_compute_images_statistics, otb_haralick_texture, otb_binary_morphological, otb_gray_scale_morphological, otb_multi_resolution_pyramid, otb_bundle_to_perfect_sensor (always); otb_radiometric_indices (invalid -list value / silent all-zero output)
- **Trigger (PROVO-4)**: run Extract ROI with any non-degenerate extent

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (PROVO-1)**: wrappers emit the real OTB application names and parameter keys so the 30 registered toolbox tools execute
- **Expected (PROVO-4)**: ulx=xmin, uly=ymax, lrx=xmax, lry=ymin with -mode.extent.unit phy (or use QgsProcessingParameters::parameterAsExtent)

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (PROVO-1)**: 4 apps cannot start ('OTB application not found'), 8 apps fail at OTB argument parsing ('Cannot find parameter ...' / invalid -mode value), radiometric_indices produces garbage or fails
- **Actual (PROVO-4)**: crossed lrx/lry values interpreted as pixel offsets - wrong, empty, or out-of-range ROI

## Root cause
- **PROVO-1**: CONFIRMED (13 of 30 registered wrappers verified broken): (a) 4 nonexistent OTB app names - LSMS, FeatureExtraction, GrayLevelCooccurrenceMatrix, PixelInfo all return HTTP 404 for their cookbook app pages (real apps: Segmentation + LSMSVectorization, HaralickTextureExtraction, PixelValue); (b) 9 grammar-broken: otb_segmentation emits -mode meanshift/-mode.meanshift.spatialr (OTB: -mode=[vector|raster], -filter.meanshift.spatialr), otb_concatenate_images -in (OTB -il), otb_compute_images_statistics -out (OTB out.xml - verified via cookbook), otb_haralick_texture -parameters.radius (OTB parameters.xrad/yrad), otb_binary/gray_scale_morphological -struct <int> + missing mandatory -channel (OTB -structype [box|ball|cross], -xradius/-yradius), otb_multi_resolution_pyramid -levels/-method (OTB -level/-sfactor), otb_bundle_to_perfect_sensor -in/-pan (OTB -inxs/-inp), otb_radiometric_indices -list NDVI + never sets -channels.* (defaults band 1 -> (b1-b1)/(b1+b1) garbage). All 13 registered in OtbToolsProvider::loadAlgorithms (provider.cpp:54-98) and reachable via the Processing toolbox. test_otb_segmentation_params.cpp:105-119 asserts the wrong grammar, masking the bug.
  - *Callchain*: `QgisAlgorithmsProvider/OtbToolsProvider::loadAlgorithms -> toolbox dialog -> OtbToolWrapper::processAlgorithm (otb_tool_wrapper.cpp:72) -> buildArgs() -> QProcess otbcli_<App> -> OTB CLI parser rejects unknown key or app binary not found`
  - *Code context*: `(a) Nonexistent apps: applicationName() returns 'LSMS' (otb_lsms.h:16), 'FeatureExtraction', 'GrayLevelCooccurrenceMatrix', 'PixelInfo' - none exist in OTB (cookbook app_LSMS/FeatureExtraction/GrayLevelCooccurrenceMatrix/PixelInfo = HTTP 404; the real apps are MeanShiftSmoothing+LSMSVectorization, HaralickTextureExtraction, PixelValue). ToolPathManager::otbToolPath('LSMS') finds no otbcli_LSMS -> processAlgorithm throws 'OTB application not found'. (b) Invented keys (verified against orfeo-toolbox.org cookbook): otb_segmentation.cpp emits '-mode meanshift' but OTB Segmentation '-mode' choices are [vector|raster] (algorithm choice is '-filter' [meanshift|cc|watershed|mprofiles], no 'lsms'); '-mode.meanshift.spatialr' should be 'filter.meanshift.spatialr'; '-out vec.shp label.tif uint32' should be '-mode.vector.out'/'-mode.raster.out' (single output). otb_concatenate_images.cpp emits '-in' but ConcatenateImages key is 'il' (list). otb_compute_images_statistics.cpp emits '-out' for the stats XML but the key is 'out.xml'. otb_haralick_texture.cpp emits '-parameters.radius' but HaralickTextureExtraction has parameters.xrad/yrad (no .radius). otb_binary_morphological.cpp/otb_gray_scale_morphological.cpp emit '-struct <int>' but the real key is '-structype [box|ball|cross]' with '-xradius/-yradius', and mandatory '-channel' is never passed. otb_multi_resolution_pyramid.cpp emits '-levels' and '-method nearest|gaussian|mean' but MultiResolutionPyramid keys are '-level','-sfactor' (no -method). otb_bundle_to_perfect_sensor.cpp emits '-in'/'-pan' but BundleToPerfectSensor keys are 'inxs'/'inp'. otb_radiometric_indices.cpp emits '-list NDVI' (valid values are 'Vegetation:NDVI' style) and never sets '-channels.*' so all channels default to 1 -> NDVI=(b1-b1)/(b1+b1). tests/test_otb_segmentation_params.cpp:105-119 asserts the wrong -mode grammar, masking the defect.`
- **PROVO-4**: CONFIRMED (otb_extract_roi.cpp:24-33): QGIS extent 'xmin,xmax,ymin,ymax' mapped to lrx=extent[2]=ymin, lry=extent[1]=xmax (swapped); no '-mode.extent.unit' passed and OTB default unit is pxl (pixel) - map-unit coords parsed as pixel indices (cookbook-verified default 'pxl'); .toString() on a QgsRectangle QVariant (programmatic batch paths) yields empty -> extent silently dropped.
  - *Callchain*: `OtbToolsProvider -> otb_extract_roi run -> buildArgs -> '-mode.extent.lrx <ymin>' '-mode.extent.lry <xmax>' -> otbcli_ExtractROI builds ROI with crossed corner coordinates in pixel units`
  - *Code context*: `'QStringList extent = parameters.value("EXTENT").toString().split(","); if (extent.size()==4) { args << "-mode" << "extent" << "-mode.extent.ulx" << extent[0] << "-mode.extent.uly" << extent[3] << "-mode.extent.lrx" << extent[2] << "-mode.extent.lry" << extent[1]; }'. QGIS extent string order is xmin,xmax,ymin,ymax so extent[1]=xmax and extent[2]=ymin; OTB ExtractROI expects lrx=lower-right X (xmax) and lry=lower-right Y (ymin) - the two assignments are swapped. Additionally '-mode.extent.unit' defaults to pxl (pixels) in OTB and the wrapper never passes 'phy', so map-unit coordinates are parsed as pixel indices. The guard also uses .toString() on the EXTENT variant, which returns an empty string when the value is a QgsRectangle QVariant (programmatic/batch paths), silently dropping the extent and leaving default 'standard' mode.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Tried to refute the 'invented keys' claims per-app against the OTB cookbook: Segmentation (mode/filter grammars, meanshift spatial radius keys), ConcatenateImages (-il), ComputeImagesStatistics (out.xml), HaralickTextureExtraction (parameters.xrad/yrad, -channel), BinaryMorphologicalOperation (-structype/-xradius, mandatory -channel), MultiResolutionPyramid (-level/-sfactor, no -method), BundleToPerfectSensor (-inxs/-inp), RadiometricIndices (-list 'Vegetation:NDVI' style, channels default 1), ExtractROI (mode.extent.*, unit default pxl). All matched the finding. Nonexistent-app claims confirmed by app_*.html 404s (LSMS, FeatureExtraction, GrayLevelCooccurrenceMatrix) and PixelValue-vs-PixelInfo. execute-path (otb_tool_wrapper.cpp:72 otbToolPath->'OTB application ... not found' throw; buildArgs -> otbcli rejects unknown key) verified.
- **Review note**: otb_ref/ in the repo has NO Modules/Applications tree (trimmed copy: only CI/Data/Docker/Documentation/Examples/Packaging/SuperBuild/Utilities), so parameter keys could not be verified against the vendored tree as the brief assumed; all keys verified against the official orfeo-toolbox.org cookbook instead. Group severity P1 -> P2: 12 wrappers fail loudly (app-not-found / OTB argument errors, no data loss/corruption), the silent-garbage case is otb_radiometric_indices (constant/all-b1 output) which is the most dangerous member. ComputeImagesStatistics '-out'-vs-'out.xml' claim initially doubted was confirmed TRUE by the cookbook.
- **Finding PROVO-1**: `(a) Nonexistent apps: applicationName() returns 'LSMS' (otb_lsms.h:16), 'FeatureExtraction', 'GrayLevelCooccurrenceMatrix', 'PixelInfo' - none exist in OTB (cookbook app_LSMS/FeatureExtraction/GrayLevelCooccurrenceMatrix/PixelInfo = HTTP 404; the real apps are MeanShiftSmoothing+LSMSVectorization, HaralickTextureExtraction, PixelValue). ToolPathManager::otbToolPath('LSMS') finds no otbcli_LSMS -> processAlgorithm throws 'OTB application not found'. (b) Invented keys (verified against orfeo-toolbox.org cookbook): otb_segmentation.cpp emits '-mode meanshift' but OTB Segmentation '-mode' choices are [vector|raster] (algorithm choice is '-filter' [meanshift|cc|watershed|mprofiles], no 'lsms'); '-mode.meanshift.spatialr' should be 'filter.meanshift.spatialr'; '-out vec.shp label.tif uint32' should be '-mode.vector.out'/'-mode.raster.out' (single output). otb_concatenate_images.cpp emits '-in' but ConcatenateImages key is 'il' (list). otb_compute_images_statistics.cpp emits '-out' for the stats XML but the key is 'out.xml'. otb_haralick_texture.cpp emits '-parameters.radius' but HaralickTextureExtraction has parameters.xrad/yrad (no .radius). otb_binary_morphological.cpp/otb_gray_scale_morphological.cpp emit '-struct <int>' but the real key is '-structype [box|ball|cross]' with '-xradius/-yradius', and mandatory '-channel' is never passed. otb_multi_resolution_pyramid.cpp emits '-levels' and '-method nearest|gaussian|mean' but MultiResolutionPyramid keys are '-level','-sfactor' (no -method). otb_bundle_to_perfect_sensor.cpp emits '-in'/'-pan' but BundleToPerfectSensor keys are 'inxs'/'inp'. otb_radiometric_indices.cpp emits '-list NDVI' (valid values are 'Vegetation:NDVI' style) and never sets '-channels.*' so all channels default to 1 -> NDVI=(b1-b1)/(b1+b1). tests/test_otb_segmentation_params.cpp:105-119 asserts the wrong -mode grammar, masking the defect.`
- **Finding PROVO-4**: `'QStringList extent = parameters.value("EXTENT").toString().split(","); if (extent.size()==4) { args << "-mode" << "extent" << "-mode.extent.ulx" << extent[0] << "-mode.extent.uly" << extent[3] << "-mode.extent.lrx" << extent[2] << "-mode.extent.lry" << extent[1]; }'. QGIS extent string order is xmin,xmax,ymin,ymax so extent[1]=xmax and extent[2]=ymin; OTB ExtractROI expects lrx=lower-right X (xmax) and lry=lower-right Y (ymin) - the two assignments are swapped. Additionally '-mode.extent.unit' defaults to pxl (pixels) in OTB and the wrapper never passes 'phy', so map-unit coordinates are parsed as pixel indices. The guard also uses .toString() on the EXTENT variant, which returns an empty string when the value is a QgsRectangle QVariant (programmatic/batch paths), silently dropping the extent and leaving default 'standard' mode.`

## Suggested fix
- Align parameter definitions, argument builders, and result harvesting with the QGIS/GDAL/OTB provider execution contract.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (PROVO-1, PROVO-4).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- #172 (closed, P1) = 'OTB wrappers pass invented parameter names - six verified applications can never run', fixed by 8cc594609a for band_math/mean_shift_smoothing/ortho_rectification/rescale/superimpose/train_vector_classifier only; PROVO-1 is the verified incomplete-fix remainder (same defect family, 13 further apps) - not a dupe of an outstanding issue but a re-open/follow-up of a closed one. #196 file-destination outputs is a different bug; #240 otb_median_filter.json unrelated.

ISSUE #298: [P2][data/crs-nodata-metadata] Headless rs: spectral-index/band-math/terrain operators still read raw DN without NoData masking (#192 fixed only the QGIS provider path)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Headless rs: spectral-index/band-math/terrain operators still read raw DN without NoData masking (#192 fixed only the QGIS provider path). This issue aggregates 2 related finding(s): OPS-1, RSALGO-8. Specifically, Headless rs:spectral_index / rs:*_index / rs:band_math / rs:terrain_analysis read raw DN without NoData masking and write outputs with undeclared NoData. Specifically, rs:band_math operator path still reads raw DN with no NoData masking and writes no NoData - #192 fix covered only the QGIS provider path.

## Area
Data Model / Raster Grids / NoData & CRS

## Type
data/crs-nodata-metadata

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/operators/rs/rs_spectral_index_operator.cpp:208-281 (spectral index); src/processing/algorithms/band_math.cpp:608-651; src/operators/rs/rs_terrain_analysis_operator.cpp:130-172; src/processing/gdal/gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:507-547` (spectral_index_detail::runSpectralIndexCore / BandMath::processFile / RsTerrainAnalysisOperator::run / writeGdalOutput)
- `src/processing/algorithms/band_math.cpp:608-651` (BandMath::processFile)

## User impact
- Silent science corruption of the flagship NDVI/indices path on every masked scene: clouds/fill turn into plausible index values; downstream operators that honor declared NoData (kmeans, rx_anomaly, apply_mask) and QGIS display treat sentinel pixels as valid data.
- rs:* operator/pipeline users (the agent/MCP surface) get different, wrong results from the identical expression run through the QGIS provider surface - same class as #192, incompletely fixed.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (OPS-1)**: Any rs:spectral_index/rs:ndvi/rs:band_math/rs:terrain_analysis run on a raster that declares a numeric band NoData (e.g. MODIS -3000 fill, Landsat fill 0, or any -9999 product) or that contains NaN/edge NoData pixels.
- **Trigger (RSALGO-8)**: rs:band_math on a raster with any declared NoData sentinel (e.g. expression '(b1-b2)/(b1+b2)' over a scene with nodata=0)

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (OPS-1)**: Input NoData pixels propagate to the output as a declared sentinel (band NoData set on the written GeoTIFF), matching what the #192/#202 fixes did for the QGIS provider path and for SAM/RX/mosaic outputs.
- **Expected (RSALGO-8)**: NoData pixels masked to NoData in inputs and output (as the provider algorithm now does)

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (OPS-1)**: NoData DNs are fed into the index formulas (fill becomes a computed index value, e.g. -9999/-9999 -> 0) and the output Float32 bands carry NaN or the raw sentinel with no GDAL band NoData declaration.
- **Actual (RSALGO-8)**: Sentinel values enter the expression arithmetic (0 gives div-by-zero NaN by luck; -9999 gives wildly wrong index values) and output NaNs are undeclared

## Root cause
- **OPS-1**: CONFIRMED for spectral-index and band-math components (readBandData gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:186-200 is a plain GDALRasterIO float read, no NoData->NaN; rs_spectral_index_operator.cpp:208-281 readBand lambda uses it for all indices; ndvi via MathUtils::safeDiv gives -9999/-9999 -> 0.0 plausible value). REFUTED for the terrain component as stated: rs_terrain_analysis_operator.cpp:113-122 DOES resolve input NoData correctly (bandNoDataValue(1,&hasNodata), only overrides -9999 default when hasNodata&&finite) and passes it to the kernels - terrain's actual defect is only the undeclared output sentinel (already C08/RSALGO-4), not raw-DN reading.
  - *Callchain*: `MCP execute_algorithm / CLI -> JobEngine 'processing:' executor (src/app/shell/processing_job_adapter.cpp:64 adapter->execute) -> AtomicAlgorithmRegistry -> RsOperatorAdapter::execute (atomic_algorithm_adapter.cpp:302) -> RSOperator::run -> runSpectralIndexCore / BandMath::processFile; also asset_index_pipeline.cpp:112 op->execute for the GUI asset path`
  - *Code context*: `readBand() calls ds.readBandData() which is a plain GDALRasterIO float conversion (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:186-200, no NoData->NaN). Output goes through writeGdalOutput() (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:507-547) which never calls GDALSetRasterNoDataValue. Spectral indices emit NaN via MathUtils::safeDiv on 0/0 but leave the sentinel undeclared; a -9999 NoData pair gives NDVI = 0/(-19998) = 0.0 (a valid-looking value). Terrain kernels write the input `nodata` sentinel (default -9999) at image borders into the Float32 output, also undeclared. BandMath::processFile reads every band via readBandData (band_math.cpp:624-632) with the same masking gap and writes via writeGdalOutput (641-648).`
- **RSALGO-8**: CONFIRMED (band_math.cpp:608-651: readBandData raw DN for every band, evaluate, writeGdalOutput; zero NoData handling in file; rs_band_math_operator.cpp:89 calls processFile; provider contrast band_math_algorithm.cpp:100/133 masks and declares - #192 asymmetry real)
  - *Callchain*: `rs:band_math (rs_band_math_operator.cpp:89) -> BandMath::processFile:608 -> readBandData (raw DN) -> evaluate -> writeGdalOutput (no NoData declared)`
  - *Code context*: `processFile: `srcDataset.readBandData(i, buffer.data(), ...)` for every band, evaluate(), then `writeGdalOutput(...)` - zero NoData handling anywhere in the file (grep: 8 NaN matches, all expression-eval NaN semantics). The #192 fix commit 0169bd7eac added masking to the QGIS provider algorithms (band_math_algorithm.cpp:100-101 converts isNoData->NaN, :133 declares output NoData) but not to BandMath::processFile used by the rs: operator. rs_band_math_operator.cpp:89 calls processFile directly.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Checked whether #192's commit also fixed the rs: operator path: git show --stat 0169bd7eac touched providers spectral_index_algorithm.cpp/band_math_algorithm.cpp/raster_ndvi.cpp but NOT src/operators/rs/* or src/processing/algorithms/band_math.cpp - asymmetry confirmed at HEAD by direct read. Verified provider paths mask (spectral_index_algorithm.cpp:61-62 isNoData->NaN, :229-241 declare output NoData).
- **Review note**: OPS-1 title/scope overstates terrain: move the terrain raw-DN claim to C08's output-declaration defect. OPS-1 cited lines for terrain (130-172) show kernels receiving resolved nodata, contradicting the 'reads raw DN without NoData masking' framing for that member.
- **Finding OPS-1**: `readBand() calls ds.readBandData() which is a plain GDALRasterIO float conversion (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:186-200, no NoData->NaN). Output goes through writeGdalOutput() (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:507-547) which never calls GDALSetRasterNoDataValue. Spectral indices emit NaN via MathUtils::safeDiv on 0/0 but leave the sentinel undeclared; a -9999 NoData pair gives NDVI = 0/(-19998) = 0.0 (a valid-looking value). Terrain kernels write the input `nodata` sentinel (default -9999) at image borders into the Float32 output, also undeclared. BandMath::processFile reads every band via readBandData (band_math.cpp:624-632) with the same masking gap and writes via writeGdalOutput (641-648).`
- **Finding RSALGO-8**: `processFile: `srcDataset.readBandData(i, buffer.data(), ...)` for every band, evaluate(), then `writeGdalOutput(...)` - zero NoData handling anywhere in the file (grep: 8 NaN matches, all expression-eval NaN semantics). The #192 fix commit 0169bd7eac added masking to the QGIS provider algorithms (band_math_algorithm.cpp:100-101 converts isNoData->NaN, :133 declares output NoData) but not to BandMath::processFile used by the rs: operator. rs_band_math_operator.cpp:89 calls processFile directly.`

## Suggested fix
- Validate grid compatibility rigorously, propagate NoData masks across all processing steps, and preserve CRS/metadata on output datasets.

ISSUE #300: [P2][algorithm/numerics] Radiometric calibration processFile treats NoData-unset bands as NoData=0 and NaNs every valid DN=0 pixel (deterministic, test-proven)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Radiometric calibration processFile treats NoData-unset bands as NoData=0 and NaNs every valid DN=0 pixel (deterministic, test-proven). Specifically, Radiometric calibration processFile treats NoData-unset as NoData=0 and NaNs out every valid DN=0 pixel (baseline failure a is a real product defect).

## Area
Algorithms / Image Processing / Numerics

## Type
algorithm/numerics

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/processing/algorithms/radiometric_calibration.cpp:530,554-558` (RadiometricCalibration::processFile)

## User impact
- Silent data corruption in the core DN->radiance/reflectance/brightness-temperature path; all three OutputUnit modes share the same clause. Fix: use the hasNodata out-param (only match bandNoData when hasNodata==true).

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (TESTS-1)**: Any input raster whose band declares no NoData value (typical for raw Landsat/Sentinel stacks produced by third-party tools or by createOutputTiff itself) and contains DN==0 pixels. Deterministic: ~1/256 pixels of uniformly distributed 8-bit data.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (TESTS-1)**: With NoData unset, DN==0 is valid data: result = gain*0 + bias = 0.0 (what the test asserts).

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (TESTS-1)**: Result pixel = NaN; output band NoData is declared NaN (line 524), so downstream stats/mosaics silently drop ~0.4% of valid pixels per 8-bit band.

## Root cause
- **TESTS-1**: CONFIRMED with live reproduction: ran audit-build tests/test_radiometric_calibration with QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen -> 'processFile streams a multi-tile raster to radiance' (test_radiometric_calibration.cpp:519) FAILS at :561 'nanf is within 0.001 of 0.0'; 19/20 pass, deterministic. Root cause exactly as filed: radiometric_calibration.cpp:530 calls 1-arg bandNoDataValue(b) (hasNodata out-param dropped, wrapper gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:385-402 returns GDALGetRasterNoDataValue verbatim = 0.0 when unset); :556 clause |v-bandNoData|<1e-4 NaNs every DN==0 pixel; fixture band=(x+3y)%256 has band[0]==0 at (0,0); createOutputTiff sets no NoData so input is unset; contrast readBandWindow :219-226 uses hasNodata correctly with a comment stating the violated invariant.
  - *Callchain*: `processFile -> GdalDatasetWrapper::bandNoDataValue(b) [ignores hasNodata] -> tile loop converts v==0 to NaN -> outDataset.writeBandWindow`
  - *Code context*: `Line 530: const double bandNoData = srcDataset.bandNoDataValue(b); — the 1-arg call ignores the hasNodata out-param. GdalDatasetWrapper::bandNoDataValue (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:385-402) returns GDALGetRasterNoDataValue verbatim: 0.0 with hasNodataInt=0 when the band declares no NoData. Line 556 then applies: if (!std::isfinite(v) || (!std::isnan(bandNoData) && std::abs(v - bandNoData) < 1e-4f)) out[i] = quiet_NaN(); — with bandNoData==0.0, every pixel with |v|<1e-4 (i.e. every DN==0 pixel) is written as NaN. The failing test test_radiometric_calibration.cpp:519 'processFile streams a multi-tile raster to radiance' writes DN=(x+3y)%256 (band[0]==0 at pixel (0,0)) and asserts result[i]==0.01*band[i]; rerun of the audit-build binary fails deterministically at i=0: 'nanf is within 0.001 of 0.0' (20 test cases, 1 failed, reproducible). Tiling is NOT the trigger — the same bug fires on any raster with a 0-valued DN pixel and unset NoData; the sibling single-tile test (test_radiometric_calibration.cpp:354) only passes because its fixture DNs are 100/200/50/80 (no zeros). The wrapper's own readBandWindow (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:219-226) uses the hasNodata flag correctly and its comment states the violated invariant: 'zero is a real pixel value for DN imagery and silently corrupts downstream statistics'. The test suite never pins this contract: test_gdal_wrapper.cpp:245-256 'reads band no-data value' does (void)nodata; (void)hasNodata; REQUIRE(true) — a tautology that let the misuse through.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Could not refute: deterministic failing baseline test is direct evidence; tiling NOT the trigger (single-tile sibling passes only because fixture DNs 100/200/50/80 contain no zero); test_gdal_wrapper tautology (REQUIRE(true)) verified as filed.
- **Review note**: Add sibling site: atmospheric_correction.cpp:406 has the identical 1-arg misuse with the same :426 clause (DN==0 -> NaN when NoData unset) - same fix needed there. Output band IS declared NaN-NoData (:524) so the corruption is silent drop of ~1/256 uniformly-distributed 8-bit pixels, as filed.
- **Finding TESTS-1**: `Line 530: const double bandNoData = srcDataset.bandNoDataValue(b); — the 1-arg call ignores the hasNodata out-param. GdalDatasetWrapper::bandNoDataValue (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:385-402) returns GDALGetRasterNoDataValue verbatim: 0.0 with hasNodataInt=0 when the band declares no NoData. Line 556 then applies: if (!std::isfinite(v) || (!std::isnan(bandNoData) && std::abs(v - bandNoData) < 1e-4f)) out[i] = quiet_NaN(); — with bandNoData==0.0, every pixel with |v|<1e-4 (i.e. every DN==0 pixel) is written as NaN. The failing test test_radiometric_calibration.cpp:519 'processFile streams a multi-tile raster to radiance' writes DN=(x+3y)%256 (band[0]==0 at pixel (0,0)) and asserts result[i]==0.01*band[i]; rerun of the audit-build binary fails deterministically at i=0: 'nanf is within 0.001 of 0.0' (20 test cases, 1 failed, reproducible). Tiling is NOT the trigger — the same bug fires on any raster with a 0-valued DN pixel and unset NoData; the sibling single-tile test (test_radiometric_calibration.cpp:354) only passes because its fixture DNs are 100/200/50/80 (no zeros). The wrapper's own readBandWindow (gdal_dataset_wrapper.cpp:219-226) uses the hasNodata flag correctly and its comment states the violated invariant: 'zero is a real pixel value for DN imagery and silently corrupts downstream statistics'. The test suite never pins this contract: test_gdal_wrapper.cpp:245-256 'reads band no-data value' does (void)nodata; (void)hasNodata; REQUIRE(true) — a tautology that let the misuse through.`

## Suggested fix
- Correct the mathematical formulas, coordinate system transforms, and NoData boundary conditions to align with standard GIS conventions (Horn 1981, GDAL, IEEE-754).

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (TESTS-1).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- #193 (line 73) is the opposite direction (ignoring DECLARED NoData); #192/#190 unrelated; no closed issue covers unset-NoData-as-0. Not a dupe.
- Finding TESTS-1: Not a dup: #193 covered atomic processors IGNORING declared NoData (opposite direction); #192/#190 covered missing masking in index/bandmath; none covered treating UNSET NoData as 0 in radiometric calibration.

ISSUE #302: [P2][algorithm/numerics] Generic GDAL-metadata TOA reflectance inverts scale semantics (phys = DN*scale+offset, not (DN+offset)/scale)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Generic GDAL-metadata TOA reflectance inverts scale semantics (phys = DN*scale+offset, not (DN+offset)/scale). Specifically, Generic (GDAL-metadata) TOA reflectance inverts scale semantics: rho = (DN+offset)/scale instead of GDAL's phys = DN*scale + offset.

## Area
Algorithms / Image Processing / Numerics

## Type
algorithm/numerics

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/processing/algorithms/radiometric_calibration.cpp:296-307, 403-409` (RadiometricCalibration::loadGdalMetadata / toToaReflectance)

## User impact
- Silently wrong reflectances by up to 8 orders of magnitude on the generic sensor path; downstream indices, change detection and the SICNU_RADIOMETRIC_STATE=comparable check all operate on garbage values.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (RSALGO-2)**: Radiometric calibration to TOA reflectance on a raster without MTL/MTD whose bands expose GDAL SCALE/OFFSET (MODIS HDF4/HDF5/NetCDF, scaled GeoTIFFs) - e.g. MOD09 with scale_factor 0.0001

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (RSALGO-2)**: phys = DN * scale + offset (GDAL convention) -> MODIS DN 5000 with scale 1e-4 -> 0.5 reflectance

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (RSALGO-2)**: (DN + offset)/scale -> 5000/1e-4 = 5e7

## Root cause
- **RSALGO-2**: CONFIRMED (toToaReflectance generic branch radiometric_calibration.cpp:403-409 computes (dn+offset)*invScale; S2 loader sets c.scale=QUANTIFICATION_VALUE (~10000) at :250 so divide is correct for S2; loadGdalMetadata :296-297 sets c.scale=GDALGetRasterScale which per current official GDAL docs means 'Units value = (raw value * scale) + offset' (verified against gdal.org API docs; system GDAL 3.13.2) - for MODIS scale 1e-4 the code yields DN*1e4 instead of DN*1e-4, off by 1/scale^2=1e8; offset also applied before scaling instead of after; reachable: loadMetadata falls back to loadGdalMetadata when no MTL/MTD sibling, operator auto-detect returns empty and processFile still loads via fallback)
  - *Callchain*: `rs_radiometric_calibration operator (method=toa_reflectance, no MTL/MTD present) -> RadiometricCalibration::processFile:534-548 -> toToaReflectance generic branch; coefficients from loadGdalMetadata (auto-fallback when loadMetadata has empty metadataPath)`
  - *Code context*: `loadGdalMetadata stores GDAL band scale into c.scale: `c.scale = GDALGetRasterScale(band, &hasScale)`; toToaReflectance generic branch computes `reflectance[i] = (dn[i] + offset) * invScale;` with `invScale = 1.0 / c.scale`. The Sentinel-2 quantification convention (DN/10000, scale=10000) was generalized to GDAL scale_factor, but GDAL SCALE/OFFSET semantics are physical = raw * scale + offset (multiplicative scale, offset added after). For a MODIS HDF (scale_factor=1e-4, header comment says 'Generic/MODIS fallback'): computed = DN/1e-4 = DN*10000, correct = DN*1e-4 -> output inflated by 1/(scale^2) = 1e8. Additive offset is also applied before division instead of after multiplication.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Checked historical GDAL doc formula variants ('value=(raw+offset)*scale' in old docs) - even that reading contradicts the code's division; the current documented formula (raw*scale+offset, matching CF scale_factor/add_offset conventions used by MODIS/NetCDF drivers) is authoritative for the vendored/linked 3.13.2.
- **Review note**: None. Note the fix must keep the S2 divisor convention while multiplying for GDAL-loaded scale (or normalize GDAL scale to 1/scale at load time).
- **Finding RSALGO-2**: `loadGdalMetadata stores GDAL band scale into c.scale: `c.scale = GDALGetRasterScale(band, &hasScale)`; toToaReflectance generic branch computes `reflectance[i] = (dn[i] + offset) * invScale;` with `invScale = 1.0 / c.scale`. The Sentinel-2 quantification convention (DN/10000, scale=10000) was generalized to GDAL scale_factor, but GDAL SCALE/OFFSET semantics are physical = raw * scale + offset (multiplicative scale, offset added after). For a MODIS HDF (scale_factor=1e-4, header comment says 'Generic/MODIS fallback'): computed = DN/1e-4 = DN*10000, correct = DN*1e-4 -> output inflated by 1/(scale^2) = 1e8. Additive offset is also applied before division instead of after multiplication.`

## Suggested fix
- Correct the mathematical formulas, coordinate system transforms, and NoData boundary conditions to align with standard GIS conventions (Horn 1981, GDAL, IEEE-754).

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (RSALGO-2).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- #127/#128 covered MTL/MTD parsing only; no closed issue on GDAL scale semantics. Clean.
- Finding RSALGO-2: #127/#128 covered Landsat MTL and Sentinel-2 MTD parsing only; issues-closed.txt has no GDAL scale/offset entry. Current HEAD verified: formula unchanged since fb6d240178.

## Baseline

- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code

(overlay combine loops))

  - *Callchain*: `toolbox -> e.g. qgis_algorithms:native_difference -> parameterAsSource(INPUT/OVERLAY) -> overlay geometries combined in overlay's native CRS -> feat.geometry().difference(overlayCombined) mixes coordinates from two CRSs`
  - *Code context*: `native_difference.h combines overlay geometries directly: 'overlayCombined = overlayCombined.combine(overlayFeat.geometry());' with no QgsCoordinateTransform and no CRS equality check; sink is created with source->sourceCrs(). Contrast native_clip.h:48-70 and vector_clip.cpp:48-54 which DO build 'QgsCoordinateTransform ct(overlay->sourceCrs(), source->sourceCrs(), ...)' - the #194 fix (0169bd7eac) only touched native_clip.h and native_clip_raster.h (verified via git show --stat). vector_merge.cpp likewise writes all layers' features into a sink created with layers.first()->crs() without reprojecting later layers. The select/extract-by-location pair builds a QgsSpatialIndex from untransformed INTERSECT features.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Intent angle tried: QGIS upstream native overlay algorithms transform overlay to input CRS, and this repo's own #194 fix established that contract for clip - silence here is omission, not policy. Sink created with source->sourceCrs() confirms outputs land in input CRS while overlay coords were never converted.
- **Review note**: None.
- **Finding PROVO-3**: `native_difference.h combines overlay geometries directly: 'overlayCombined = overlayCombined.combine(overlayFeat.geometry());' with no QgsCoordinateTransform and no CRS equality check; sink is created with source->sourceCrs(). ISSUE #306: [P2][concurrency/lifecycle] TaskCenter terminal-state regression: racing progress/log records regress Completed/Failed tasks to permanent Running zombies
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
TaskCenter terminal-state regression: racing progress/log records regress Completed/Failed tasks to permanent Running zombies. Specifically, updateTaskProgress/markTaskRunning only guard Canceled - a terminal (Completed/Failed) task is regressed back to Running by a racing progress/log record, leaving a permanent zombie that consumes a concurrency slot.

## Area
Concurrency / Job Engine / Lifecycle

## Type
concurrency/lifecycle

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/processing/framework/task_center.cpp:549-582, 817-829, 843-854` (TaskCenter::processJobRecord / TaskCenter::updateTaskProgress / TaskCenter::markTaskRunning)

## User impact
- Each occurrence permanently burns one slot in processNextQueuedTasks' runningByProfile/totalRunning/runningTotalMb accounting (L627-634), so repeated occurrences shrink effective parallelism to zero; UI (task_center_dock refreshTaskList) shows the task running forever; retry/cancel UX degrades (cancelTask still works but waitForTask callers already returned Completed).

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (CONC-2)**: Concrete interleaving: (a) T calls JobEngine::submit, gets jobId; (b) worker W picks it up and reports progress 0.5; (c) T registers m_taskByJobId[jobId] (L511 or L792), snapshots a RUNNING record and enters processJobRecord, passing the L551 terminality check; (d) T is preempted between L553 and L824; (e) W finishes the job and its terminal notify is processed: task becomes Completed and the dedup/dispatch keys are erased; (f) T resumes, finds m_lastForwardedProgress erased (-2.0 != 0.5) and calls updateTaskProgress -> L821 guard sees Completed (not Canceled) -> L824 writes status=Running. No further job records exist (job terminal, mapping erased), so nothing ever repairs the state.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (CONC-2)**: Terminal status is final: a stale progress/log record arriving after the terminal transition is dropped (the L551 check's intent).

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (CONC-2)**: The check and the write are in different critical sections; updateTaskProgress/markTaskRunning resurrect the task to Running with progress 0.5 and duplicate every log line into logBuffer. The task is a permanent zombie Running.

## Root cause
- **CONC-2**: CONFIRMED (processJobRecord :549-553 terminality check in its own critical section, mutations in later separate sections; updateTaskProgress :817-824 guards ONLY status==Canceled then writes status=Running - regresses Completed/Failed; markTaskRunning :843-849 same; ADR 0051 line 22 'terminal mark* methods treat terminal as final' - invariant deliberately enforced only on mark*, matching the finding; the demanded interleaving is concrete and valid: T (main thread, flushPendingLaunches :804-806 catch-up or submitJobImpl :519-520) passes check, W (worker) terminal notify erases m_taskByJobId/m_lastForwardedProgress/m_forwardedLogCounts (:609-613), T resumes: value(taskId,-2.0)!=0.5 -> updateTaskProgress flips Completed->Running and the log loop re-appends every line from forwarded=0 - permanent zombie, no further records exist (mapping erased) to repair it)
  - *Callchain*: `Thread T (GUI thread in submitJobImpl L519-520, or the flushing thread in flushPendingLaunches L804-806): JobEngine::snapshot -> onJobRecord(runningRecord) -> processJobRecord [check passes] -> preempted -> Thread W (JobEngine worker): notify(terminal) -> onJobRecord -> markTaskCompleted -> Completed + m_taskByJobId.remove/m_forwardedLogCounts.remove/m_lastForwardedProgress.remove (L609-613) -> T resumes: updateTaskProgress(0.5) flips Completed->Running; log loop re-appends every log line (dedup counter was erased)`
  - *Code context*: `processJobRecord validates terminality once, in a SEPARATE critical section from the mutations it then performs:
  { QMutexLocker locker(&m_mutex);
    if ( !m_tasks.contains(taskId) || isTerminalStatus(m_tasks[taskId].status) ) return; }   // L549-553
  ... progress handling ...
  if ( changed ) updateTaskProgress( taskId, record.progress );                              // L568
updateTaskProgress then does:
  if ( !m_tasks.contains(taskId) || m_tasks[taskId].status == TaskStatus::Canceled ) return;  // L821 - Canceled ONLY
  m_tasks[taskId].progressPercentage = progress;
  m_tasks[taskId].status = TaskStatus::Running;                                              // L823-824 regresses Completed/Failed -> Running
markTaskRunning has the same Canceled-only guard (L847) and sets Running at L849. The ADR 0051 invariant 'terminal mark* methods treat terminal as final' (docs/adr/0051 L21-22) is thus only enforced on the mark* methods, not on the progress/running writers.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Checked lock scopes at HEAD: the gap between :553 and :824 spans three lock/unlock cycles - real TOCTOU window. Checked whether flushPendingLaunches re-checks terminality before applying the record: it delegates to onJobRecord->processJobRecord which has the same split. No catch-up re-snapshot of terminal jobs exists (mapping erased), so nothing repairs the zombie.
- **Review note**: None.
- **Finding CONC-2**: `processJobRecord validates terminality once, in a SEPARATE critical section from the mutations it then performs:
  { QMutexLocker locker(&m_mutex);
    if ( !m_tasks.contains(taskId) || isTerminalStatus(m_tasks[taskId].status) ) return; }   // L549-553
  ... progress handling ...
  if ( changed ) updateTaskProgress( taskId, record.progress );                              // L568
updateTaskProgress then does:
  if ( !m_tasks.contains(taskId) || m_tasks[taskId].status == TaskStatus::Canceled ) return;  // L821 - Canceled ONLY
  m_tasks[taskId].progressPercentage = progress;
  m_tasks[taskId].status = TaskStatus::Running;                                              // L823-824 regresses Completed/Failed -> Running
markTaskRunning has the same Canceled-only guard (L847) and sets Running at L849. The ADR 0051 invariant 'terminal mark* methods treat terminal as final' (docs/adr/0051 L21-22) is thus only enforced on the mark* methods, not on the progress/running writers.`

## Suggested fix
- Ensure proper synchronization, atomic state transitions, clean thread termination, and avoidance of use-after-free or race conditions on worker/GUI threads.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (CONC-2).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- No closed issue covers status regression via progress/log writers (#205-#213, #220, #233, batch-21 verified in issues-closed.txt). Clean; distinct from C57's #207 residual-watcher teardown race.
- Finding CONC-2: Not a dupe: ADR 0051 (closed) established the single-listener + catch-up design and claimed 'terminal is final'; the delta logic dedups terminal marks but not the progress/log writers. No closed issue (#205-#213, #220, #233, #237, batch-21) covers status regression.

ISSUE #308: [P2][algorithm/numerics] fit() residual engine incompatible with RPC: panel-CRS-unit destinations fed to WGS84-degree inverse; Y-flip unpaired - residuals wrong on perfect models
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
fit() residual engine incompatible with RPC: panel-CRS-unit destinations fed to WGS84-degree inverse; Y-flip unpaired - residuals wrong on perfect models. Specifically, fit() residual engine is incompatible with RpcPhysical: destination points are fed in panel-CRS units to a WGS84-degree RPC inverse, and the facade Y-flip is unpaired (invertYAxis ignored by the RPC impl), so RPC residuals/RMS are garbage (residual.y ~ 2*row) or silently invalid.

## Area
Algorithms / Image Processing / Numerics

## Type
algorithm/numerics

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/app/georeferencer/qgsgeoreftransform.cpp:52-92,216-229` (sourcePixelResiduals / QgsGeorefTransform::transformWorldToRaster)

## User impact
- RPC mode's entire quality-feedback loop (RMS label, per-point residual table, scatter plot, marker residual arrows, 精化前/后 RMS diagnostic added by #286) shows garbage or nothing. Users cannot judge an RPC fit; refinement 'improvement' display is meaningless even though the underlying bias decision (computed inside updateParametersFromGcps with raw GDAL calls) is correct.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (GEOREF-2)**: RPC mode in the I2M window with any GCPs. Case A (projected panel CRS, the default): UTM meters are passed as lon/lat degrees to GDALRPCTransform inverse -> failure or nonsense -> residuals become the NaN sentinel, 'RMS: —', refinement diagnostic cleared, no error. Case B (EPSG:4326 panel CRS): predicted.y = -line vs observed.y = +line (canvas picks store (col,-line) because the QGIS provider forces gt[5]=-1 for unreferenced rasters, src/core/providers/gdal/qgsgdalprovider.cpp:673-678; georeferenced sources get (col,line) y-down and mirror identically) -> residual.y = ~2*row even for a perfect RPC model; RMS ~ image height in pixels.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (GEOREF-2)**: Residuals computed by converting dst into the RPC model's WGS84 output space (the setDestinationCrs seam added for #286) and comparing in a consistent y convention with the stored source points, as done for linear/helmert where the test comment says 'invertY bookends cancel' (test_georeferencing_session.cpp:229).

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (GEOREF-2)**: For RpcPhysical the bookends do not cancel: residual y is mirrored (perfect model reports residuals of order 2*pixel row, e.g. hundreds of px on a 1000-row image) or all residuals are invalid. test_georeferencing_session.cpp:348-358 only asserts fit.rms >= 0 and fit.rms < fit.refinementRmsBefore - both hold equally for the mirrored values, so tests stay green; test_rpc_gcp_refine.cpp exercises the raw transformer only.

## Root cause
- **GEOREF-2**: CONFIRMED (both sub-claims hold at HEAD). Case A CRS: fit() collects dst homogenized to targetCrs = first enabled GCP's dest CRS = panel CRS, default EPSG:32650 (rs_georef_params_panel.cpp:339-343); makeConfiguredTransform does call impl->setDestinationCrs (qgsgeoreftransform.cpp:42) but QgsRpcGcpTransformer uses mDestinationCrs ONLY inside the refinement-bias block (qgsrpcgcptransformer.cpp:190-206, the #286 fix) - transform()/rpcTransformWithBias never converts, so UTM meters hit GDALRPCTransform bDstToSrc as if lon/lat -> fail/garbage -> rsGeorefInvalidResidual. Case B Y-flip: QgsRpcGcpTransformer::updateParametersFromGcps declares 'bool /*invertYAxis*/' (:121-123) so the facade's unconditional raster.setY(-raster.y()) (qgsgeoreftransform.cpp:227) is unpaired for RPC: GDALRPCTransform inverse already returns y-down line, predicted=(col,-line) vs observed=toSourcePixel=toColumnLine=(col,line) (qgsrasterchangecoords.cpp:70-78) -> residual.y ~ -2*row on a perfect model; test_georeferencing_session.cpp:348-358 only asserts rms>=0 and rms<refinementRmsBefore so garbage passes (verified assertions at :355-357)
  - *Callchain*: `RsGeoreferencingSession::refit -> QgsGeorefTransform::fit -> sourcePixelResiduals -> QgsGeorefTransform::transformWorldToRaster -> QgsGcpTransformerInterface::transform -> rpcTransformWithBias(dst->src) -> GDALRPCTransform. Then shell onSessionFitChanged (qgsgeoref_shell_window.cpp:1303-1376) shows fit.rms/residuals in panel, GCP table, scatter, marker arrows, refinement before/after labels.`
  - *Code context*: `sourcePixelResiduals (line 65): 'if ( !xf.transformWorldToRaster( dst.at( i ), predicted ) )' - dst comes from collectEnabledGcps homogenized to targetCrs (line 374), typically the panel CRS (default EPSG:32650, rs_georef_params_panel.cpp:339-341). transformWorldToRaster (line 227) unconditionally 'raster.setY( -raster.y() )', which pairs with impls that honor invertYAxis (linear/helmert/GDAL flip internally); QgsRpcGcpTransformer::updateParametersFromGcps declares 'bool /*invertYAxis*/' (qgsrpcgcptransformer.cpp:123) - GDAL's RPC natively consumes y-down pixel/line, so the facade flip is unpaired.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Primary refutation hypothesis was that setDestinationCrs (added for #286) also covers the residual path - refuted by reading qgsrpcgcptransformer.cpp: mDestinationCrs appears only in the wgsDst lambda. Second hypothesis: GDALRPCTransform might accept projected coords - refuted: GDAL RPC transformer works strictly in WGS84 lon/lat on the destination side (rpcTransformWithBias subtracts degree-unit biases confirming the contract). Unreferenced-canvas gt[5]=-1 claim verified at qgsgdalprovider.cpp:673-677.
- **Review note**: Line refs all accurate at HEAD. Sign of Case B residual is -2*row (finding says ~2*row - magnitude claim correct). The bias decision inside updateParametersFromGcps IS correct (#286), as the finding concedes - defect is strictly the residual/RMS feedback path.
- **Finding GEOREF-2**: `sourcePixelResiduals (line 65): 'if ( !xf.transformWorldToRaster( dst.at( i ), predicted ) )' - dst comes from collectEnabledGcps homogenized to targetCrs (line 374), typically the panel CRS (default EPSG:32650, rs_georef_params_panel.cpp:339-341). transformWorldToRaster (line 227) unconditionally 'raster.setY( -raster.y() )', which pairs with impls that honor invertYAxis (linear/helmert/GDAL flip internally); QgsRpcGcpTransformer::updateParametersFromGcps declares 'bool /*invertYAxis*/' (qgsrpcgcptransformer.cpp:123) - GDAL's RPC natively consumes y-down pixel/line, so the facade flip is unpaired.`

## Suggested fix
- Correct the mathematical formulas, coordinate system transforms, and NoData boundary conditions to align with standard GIS conventions (Horn 1981, GDAL, IEEE-754).

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (GEOREF-2).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- #286 (line 3) fixed bias units/median/improvement inside the transformer; no closed issue covers the residual engine's CRS-space or Y-convention mismatch. Adjacent root cause, distinct fix surface - not a dupe.
- Finding GEOREF-2: #286 fixed bias units/median/improvement-check inside QgsRpcGcpTransformer; no closed issue covers the residual engine's CRS-space or Y-convention mismatch with RPC. Adjacent-but-distinct root cause in the same feature area.

ISSUE #310: [P2][correctness] #246 incomplete: production .points save/load never passes sourceGeoTransform - map coordinates still land in pixel columns
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
#246 incomplete: production .points save/load never passes sourceGeoTransform - map coordinates still land in pixel columns. Specifically, #246 incompletely fixed: production save/load never pass sourceGeoTransform, so .points v2 still writes map coordinates (mapY negated) into pixelX/pixelY columns and QGIS v1 pixel-coord files are still misread as map coords.

## Area

## Type
correctness

## Severity
P2

## Baseline

- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## User impact
- .points files written by this app cannot be consumed by QGIS's georeferencer and vice versa; cross-session loads with a georeferenced raster silently corrupt GCP source coordinates.

In-app save/load round-trips only by symmetric accident (both sides skip conversion), but the negated mapY in the pixelY column also breaks QGIS interop.

## Root cause

  - *Callchain*: `User loads georeferenced source raster (I2I typical) -> GCP sourcePoints are canvas picks in source-layer map coords -> savePoints/closeEvent-save -> rsSaveGcpPointsFile with gt=nullptr writes mapX into 'pixelX' column and -mapY into 'pixelY' column (line 53-56).   - *Code context*: `Line 2030: 'rsSaveGcpPointsFile( path, mGeorefSession.gcps() )' (sourceGeoTransform defaults to nullptr); line 2054: 'rsLoadGcpPointsFile( path, destCrs, loaded )'; line 2083 same as 2030. The codec (qgsgcplist.cpp:24-124) only converts map<->pixel when sourceGeoTransform != nullptr. The #246 fix commit f8f79f717f changed qgsgcplist.cpp/.h and tests only - no shell call site passes the geotransform (grep: only tests/test_gcp_points_file.cpp:100 passes gt).`

## Evidence

- **Finding GEOREF-4**: `Line 2030: 'rsSaveGcpPointsFile( path, mGeorefSession.gcps() )' (sourceGeoTransform defaults to nullptr); line 2054: 'rsLoadGcpPointsFile( path, destCrs, loaded )'; line 2083 same as 2030. Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Advertised tool surface not executable: GUI copilot data:* tools unregistered; CLI cannot run gdal:/otb:/qgis: provider algorithms. This issue aggregates 2 related finding(s): AGMCP-3, AGMCP-4. - **Actual (AGMCP-4)**: CLI is restricted to rs:/opencv: RSOperators; provider ids unconditionally fail

## Root cause

  - *Callchain*: `LLM tool_calls data_list_layers -> onToolCallParsed -> ToolCallDispatcher::submit -> InteractionToolRegistry::execute -> error payload dropped by empty lambda`
  - *Code context*: `sendPrompt exports AgentToolCatalog::exportOpenAiToolDefinitions() which includes DataToolProvider's data:list_layers/data:describe_dataset/data:get_lineage (tool_catalog/data_tool_provider.cpp:8-145). ISSUE #314: [P2][concurrency/lifecycle] DataManager relocate/commitEdit/promote rebuild snapshots without parentCollectionId - collection membership silently dropped, double-adoption
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
DataManager relocate/commitEdit/promote rebuild snapshots without parentCollectionId - collection membership silently dropped, double-adoption. Specifically, relocate()/commitEdit()/promote() rebuild AssetSnapshot and silently drop parentCollectionId — collection membership lost, double-parenting possible.

## Area
Concurrency / Job Engine / Lifecycle

## Type
concurrency/lifecycle

## Severity
P2

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/data/data_manager.cpp:421-434, 662-673, 1359-1371` (DataManager::relocate / DataManager::commitEdit / DataManager::promote)

## User impact
- Broken catalog invariant, duplicated rows in the Data Manager panel, corrupted collection membership after an import dedups onto the affected source, and project-reload diagnostics/errors. Persistence itself survives (serializer writes collection->childAssetIds), so the corruption is silent until an adopt/reload path hits it.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (DATAPY-1)**: Any collection child that is relocated (missing-source relink), promoted from temporary, or whose vector edit is committed.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (DATAPY-1)**: The one-parent invariant documented at data_manager.cpp:1635-1645 ('A child has at most one parent collection') is preserved across snapshot rebuilds.

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (DATAPY-1)**: Snapshot reports a standalone asset while the collection still lists it. DataManagerPanel then renders the asset twice (under the collection via collection->childAssetIds, data_manager_panel.cpp:695, and as a top-level row because parentCollectionId() is nullopt, data_manager_panel.cpp:705-710). Worse, CollectionImportService::commit (src/processing/framework/collection_import_service.cpp:281-296) keys its dedup decision on snapshot->parentCollectionId(): the orphaned-looking child is 'adopted' into a second collection, so childAssetIds now contains it in two collections; on save/reload the serializer's second addChildToCollection fails with collection.child_already_owned and the project read reports failure.

## Root cause
- **DATAPY-1**: CONFIRMED
  - *Callchain*: `UI 重定位缺失源 (src/app/main_window_docks.cpp:487 dataManager.relocate(RelocateRequest{assetId, replacement})) → DataManager::relocate → snapshot rebuild → parentCollectionId==nullopt while CollectionRecord.childAssetIds still lists the asset (pruneChildFromCollections only runs on unload/reap). Promote path: main_window_docks.cpp:455 → DataManager::promote.`
  - *Code context*: `AssetSnapshot's constructor (src/data/data_asset.h:323-346) has no parentCollectionId parameter; the member (data_asset.h:358) is set only by addChildToCollection via direct member write (data_manager.cpp:1648 `assetIt->snapshot.m_parentCollectionId = collectionId;`). relocate() constructs `AssetSnapshot updated{ current.id(), newRevision, normalizedDescriptor, ..., current.acquisitionTime() };` then `recordIt->snapshot = std::move( updated );` (421-434) — m_parentCollectionId resets to nullopt. commitEdit (662-673) and promote (1359-1371) use the same rebuild pattern and drop it identically. unloadCollection's non-cascade path (1737-1742) clears the member explicitly, proving the member is only reachable by direct access.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: AssetSnapshot ctor (data_asset.h:323-346) has no parentCollectionId parameter; member is set only by addChildToCollection via friend direct write (data_manager.cpp:1648) and cleared by unloadCollection non-cascade (1741). All three rebuilds construct a fresh snapshot without the member, so m_parentCollectionId resets to nullopt while CollectionRecord.childAssetIds still lists the asset. Reachability verified: DataManagerPanel promoteRequested/relocateRequested -> main_window_docks.cpp:455/487, commitEdit via commitEditLease -> main_window_vector.cpp:435-445. Consequences verified in code: data_manager_panel.cpp:695 renders collection children by childAssetIds AND :707 skips top-level rows only when parentCollectionId has_value -> relocated child renders twice; collection_import_service.cpp:274-296 keys dedup/adoption on snapshot->parentCollectionId() so the orphaned-looking child gets adopted into a second collection (double-adoption).
- **Review note**: Line refs: snapshot rebuilds verified at data_manager.cpp:421-434 (relocate), 662-673 (commitEdit), 1351-1371 (promote); the cited 1359-1371 range is accurate.
- **Finding DATAPY-1**: `AssetSnapshot's constructor (src/data/data_asset.h:323-346) has no parentCollectionId parameter; the member (data_asset.h:358) is set only by addChildToCollection via direct member write (data_manager.cpp:1648 `assetIt->snapshot.m_parentCollectionId = collectionId;`). relocate() constructs `AssetSnapshot updated{ current.id(), newRevision, normalizedDescriptor, ..., current.acquisitionTime() };` then `recordIt->snapshot = std::move( updated );` (421-434) — m_parentCollectionId resets to nullopt. commitEdit (662-673) and promote (1359-1371) use the same rebuild pattern and drop it identically. unloadCollection's non-cascade path (1737-1742) clears the member explicitly, proving the member is only reachable by direct access.`

## Suggested fix
- Ensure proper synchronization, atomic state transitions, clean thread termination, and avoidance of use-after-free or race conditions on worker/GUI threads.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (DATAPY-1).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- issues-closed.txt: collection/promote/relocate feature issues (#43/#45/#47 temp-promote, #49/#50/#52/#104, #137/#138 collections) describe feature work, none flag parentCollectionId loss on snapshot rebuild. Not covered.
- Finding DATAPY-1: Grepped issues-closed.txt for relocate/promote/collection/parent — #49/#50/#52/#104 are the collection-import feature line, #43/#45/#47 the temporary/promote line; none describe membership loss on snapshot rebuild. Not covered.
