ISSUE #344: [P3][ui/interaction] Dialog minors batch 2: feedback leak per Run; failure path skips postProcess; result summaries fire after accept(); atmospheric dialog stale gain/bias on band change; typed default CRS discarded; CRS preset filter accepts hidden item
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Dialog minors batch 2: feedback leak per Run; failure path skips postProcess; result summaries fire after accept(); atmospheric dialog stale gain/bias on band change; typed default CRS discarded; CRS preset filter accepts hidden item. This issue aggregates 8 related finding(s): DLGB-2, DLGB-3, DLGB-4, DLGA-7, DLGB-8, DLGA-9, DLGB-7, DLGB-9. Specifically, SicnuAlgorithmDialog leaks one parentless QgsProcessingFeedback (plus its accumulated HTML log) on every Run. Specifically, Toolbox failure path never calls QgsProcessingAlgorithm::postProcess, violating the documented cleanup contract for failed runs. Specifically, Result-summary callbacks (post-classification transition matrix, QA-mask statistics) run after accept() has already closed the modal dialog — the advertised summary UI is never visible.

## Area
GUI / Application Shell / Dialogs

## Type
ui/interaction

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/app/dialogs/sicnu_algorithm_dialog.cpp:642-643, 707, 717, 726, 848` (SicnuAlgorithmDialog::runAlgorithm / algExecuted (mFeedback member))
- `src/app/dialogs/sicnu_algorithm_dialog.cpp:806-815` (SicnuAlgorithmDialog::runAlgorithm failure lambda)
- `src/app/dialogs/raster_processing_dialog_base.cpp:368-374 (onResult ordering); 389-396 (handleCompleted -> accept)` (RasterProcessingDialogBase::runOperatorTask onResult lambda / handleCompleted; consumers PostClassificationDialog::showResultSummary (post_classification_dialog.cpp:179-241, label '运行后在此显示变化统计摘要') and QaMaskDialog summary lambda (qa_mask_dialog.cpp:139-146, label '运行后在此显示掩膜统计'))
- `src/app/dialogs/atmospheric_dialog.cpp:59-61,108-120,122-180` (AtmosphericDialog::populateBandCombo / AtmosphericDialog::refreshMetadata)
- `src/app/dialogs/preferences_dialog.cpp:166-169 (defaultCrs), 171-178 (setDefaultCrs uses setCurrentText), 66-74 (editable combo), 231-240 (saveSettings)` (PreferencesDialog::defaultCrs / saveSettings)
- `src/app/dialogs/crs_preset_dialog.cpp:254-285,183-205` (CrsPresetDialog::filterTree / CrsPresetDialog::onTreeItemChanged)
- `src/app/dialogs/spectral_index_dialog.cpp:313-350 (runFromAsset now plain runOperatorTask), 141-142 (m_addToCanvasCheck), 71-73 (tooltip promising revision validation); header doc lines 17-23` (SpectralIndexDialog::runFromAsset / m_addToCanvasCheck)
- `src/app/dialogs/stac_browser_dialog.cpp:133-154 (populateResults); src/app/stac_client.cpp:143-204 (buildSearchUrl/search)` (StacBrowserDialog::searchCatalog / StacClient::search)

## User impact
- Unbounded slow memory growth in long toolbox sessions; incidentally, the leak is the only reason the cancel hook in DLGB-1 does not immediately crash after dialog destruction
- Temp feature sinks / partially written outputs and thread-safe-local-context state are not cleaned up on every failed toolbox run
- The headline reporting feature of both dialogs (transition matrix / mask statistics) is unreachable dead UI; users must re-derive stats externally
- Misleading coefficient display; wrong explicit gain/bias can be silently applied to a different band than the user believes they tuned
- User preference silently lost; no consumer of preferences/defaultCrs exists at HEAD (main_window/theme and tools/* are read), limiting blast radius to the dialog's own state
- Wrong CRS applied by the caller (project CRS / dialog consumer) with no visual indication; 'Recently Used' polluted
- ADR 0036/#40 provenance architecture silently lost for spectral-index outputs; Data Catalog users lose derivation records; dead checkbox misleads
- Users miss downloadable scenes; selections are unknowingly restricted to the first ~10 items

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (DLGB-2)**: Run any toolbox algorithm; repeat on the same dialog (Change Parameters -> Run again)
- **Trigger (DLGB-3)**: Run any toolbox algorithm that fails after runPrepared started (invalid input discovered mid-run, disk full while writing sinks, algorithm-thrown QgsProcessingException)
- **Trigger (DLGB-4)**: Run a post-classification comparison or QA mask job and wait for completion
- **Trigger (DLGA-7)**: Open 大气校正 with a sensor-metadata product, switch the 波段 combo to another band, observe/edit gain/bias, run
- **Trigger (DLGB-8)**: Type a CRS not in the preset list into Preferences > General > Default CRS and save
- **Trigger (DLGA-9)**: Select a CRS row, then type a filter term that excludes it, then press Enter
- **Trigger (DLGB-7)**: Run any spectral index from the dialog with a Data Manager configured
- **Trigger (DLGB-9)**: Search any collection with more matching items than the server's page size

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (DLGB-2)**: Feedback object owned (unique_ptr or deleteLater) and destroyed after the run, matching qgsprocessingbatchalgorithmdialogbase.cpp
- **Expected (DLGB-3)**: postProcess(context, feedback, /*runResult=*/false) called on the main thread so the algorithm cleans up temp outputs / transfers mLocalContext results (qgsprocessingalgorithm.cpp:788-800)
- **Expected (DLGB-4)**: Summary label (transition matrix, per-class net change, masked-percent) visible in the still-open dialog after the run
- **Expected (DLGA-7)**: Gain/bias spin values and status label refresh for the newly selected band (per the header contract 'fills gain/bias for the selected band')
- **Expected (DLGB-8)**: Typed CRS round-trips (or at minimum currentText() is used as fallback)
- **Expected (DLGA-9)**: Filtering moves selection to the first visible match or clears it (and disables OK) when the current item is hidden
- **Expected (DLGB-7)**: Transactional output commit registered as Data Asset with Derivation Record; stale-revision rejection; display honor the opt-in checkbox
- **Expected (DLGB-9)**: Pagination (follow 'next' links or expose a limit) or an explicit truncation warning

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (DLGB-2)**: A new leaked feedback per Run click; each retains signal connections to the still-alive dialog and its accumulated log text
- **Actual (DLGB-3)**: Algorithm instance destroyed without postProcess; per-instance cleanup skipped
- **Actual (DLGB-4)**: Label text is set on a dialog that accept() has already hidden and that the caller destroys immediately; the user never sees any summary; the matrix data is not logged either (handleCompleted logs only the output path)
- **Actual (DLGA-7)**: Band-1 coefficients and 'auto-filled' label persist for every band; manual edits start from the wrong band's values and are applied to the selected band
- **Actual (DLGB-8)**: Empty string stored; field shows blank on next open
- **Actual (DLGA-9)**: The invisible pre-filter EPSG is silently accepted and recorded in 'Recently Used'
- **Actual (DLGB-7)**: Raw file output only: no catalog registration, no provenance, no revision check; the checkbox does nothing (result loads regardless)
- **Actual (DLGB-9)**: First page silently presented as the complete result set

## Root cause
- **DLGB-2**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `runAlgorithm() -> createFeedback() (new parentless QgsProcessingAlgorithmDialogFeedback, connected to setInfo/setPercentage + buttonCancel) -> task completes -> algExecuted() sets mFeedback = nullptr without delete -> object leaks with its full run log (mMessageLoggedCount entries / textLog)`
  - *Code context*: `'mFeedback = createFeedback();' (line 642). The vendored base createFeedback() (src/gui/processing/qgsprocessingalgorithmdialogbase.cpp:421-435) returns 'feedback.release()' — a bare, parentless object. The dialog only ever drops the pointer: 'mFeedback = nullptr;' at lines 707, 717, 726, 848; grep shows no 'delete mFeedback' anywhere. The sibling vendored batch dialog owns the same object correctly: 'mFeedback.reset( createFeedback() );' (src/gui/processing/qgsprocessingbatchalgorithmdialogbase.cpp:66).`
- **DLGB-3**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `worker throws (e.g. QgsProcessingException mid-run after temp sinks were created) -> GuiJobHandle failure callback -> algExecuted(false, results) -> mRunState.reset() destroys algorithm instance, postProcess never invoked`
  - *Code context*: `Failure callback: '[this]( const QString &err, bool ) { bool successful = false; QVariantMap results = mRunState ? mRunState->results : QVariantMap(); ... algExecuted( successful, results ); mRunState.reset(); }' — mRunState.reset() destroys the prepared algorithm instance without postProcess even when mRunState->workerStarted is true (runPrepared was entered and threw). Contract: vendored qgsprocessingalgorithm.h:437-440 'Since QGIS 3.38, postProcess() will always be called even for unsuccessful run executions, to allow the algorithm to gracefully clean up.' Reference behavior: QgsProcessingAlgRunnerTask::finished (src/core/processing/qgsprocessingalgrunnertask.cpp:83-87) calls 'mAlgorithm->postProcess( mContext, mFeedback, result )' for both success and failure. The success lambda (780-805) does call postProcess; only the failure path skips it.`
- **DLGB-4**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Run click -> runOperatorTask('rs:post_classification_change'|'rs:qa_mask', params, onResult) -> task completes on GUI thread -> onCompleted -> handleCompleted -> accept() (dialog hidden) -> onResult -> showResultSummary writes to a hidden, about-to-be-destroyed QLabel`
  - *Code context*: `Success lambda: '[this, onResult]( const QString &outputPath, const Json::Value &resultJson ) { onCompleted( outputPath ); if ( onResult ) onResult( resultJson ); }' — onCompleted -> handleCompleted -> 'accept();' (line 395) hides the modal dialog synchronously; only afterwards does onResult populate m_summaryLabel. Both dialogs are exec()'ed stack objects: main_window_processing.cpp:288-290 'PostClassificationDialog dlg(this); dlg.populateLayers(); dlg.exec();' and the openRasterDialog<QaMaskDialog> template (main_window_processing.cpp:96-109) — the dialog is destroyed as soon as exec() returns after accept().`
- **DLGA-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `setRasterLayer → populateBandCombo → refreshMetadata (band 1 coefficients shown) → user switches 波段 to 2 → no signal wired → spins/label stale → onRun sends metadata_path (operator re-resolves per band — output correct, display wrong) or, if the user edited a spin, sends band-1's manual value applied to band 2`
  - *Code context*: `refreshMetadata() (lines 122-180) resolves per-band coefficients: `const int band = m_bandCombo->currentData().toInt(); ... meta.bands.value( band ); m_gainSpin->setValue( c.radianceGain ); ...` and is documented in the header as 'fills gain/bias for the selected band'. But setupUi never connects m_bandCombo: the only connects are m_methodCombo (line 55), m_gainSpin/m_biasSpin (102-105). refreshMetadata is called only from setRasterLayer (line 32) and populateBandCombo (line 119), both at layer-set time when the combo still holds band 1. Changing 波段 afterwards leaves the spins and the '已从 %1 自动填充 gain/bias' label showing band-1 values while the combo shows another band; if the user then edits a spin (m_coefficientsModified), the band-1 value is sent as the explicit gain for the newly selected band.`
- **DLGB-8**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `user types e.g. EPSG:32652 into Default CRS -> OK -> onAccept -> saveSettings -> settings.setValue("preferences/defaultCrs", "") -> reopen dialog -> loadSettings reads "" -> setDefaultCrs("") -> findData fails -> setCurrentText("")`
  - *Code context*: `m_crsCombo is editable ('m_crsCombo->setEditable(true);', line 72) and setDefaultCrs explicitly supports arbitrary text ('else m_crsCombo->setCurrentText(crs);'). But defaultCrs() returns 'm_crsCombo->currentData().toString();' — for a user-typed entry no item data exists, currentData() is an invalid QVariant and toString() is empty. saveSettings() then persists 'preferences/defaultCrs' = "".`
- **DLGA-9**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `user selects EPSG A → types a search string that hides A and reveals only B → no currentItemChanged fires (hiding is not a selection change) → Enter/OK → CrsPresets::addRecentCrs(A); selectedEpsg() returns A`
  - *Code context*: `filterTree only toggles `childItem->setHidden( !match )` and hides empty categories; it never clears or moves the current selection, and QTreeWidget keeps currentItem (and the OK button state from the last onTreeItemChanged) on hidden items. m_okButton is `setDefault(true)` (line 94), so pressing Enter after typing a filter accepts whatever m_selectedEpsg currently holds — possibly an item hidden by the filter.`
- **DLGB-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `openSpectralIndexDialog (Data Manager present) -> setDataManager -> user picks asset, Run -> onRun -> runFromAsset -> plain runOperatorTask; result auto-loaded unconditionally by the caller on accept (main_window_processing.cpp:194-198), checkbox ignored`
  - *Code context*: `Header promises: 'the run resolves the AssetRef through the Processing Asset Resolver (Task lease for the run, stale revision rejected) and commits the output transactionally through the OutputCommitter — registered as a Data Asset with a Derivation Record'. Current runFromAsset is just 'json["input"] = inputPath...; runOperatorTask( QStringLiteral( "rs:spectral_index" ), json );'. Git: commit b1fd4dce24 implemented the resolver+committer path for #40; commit 8f57fbbd79 (fix for #219) deleted it ('- sicnu::data::ProcessingAssetResolver resolver( m_dataManager ); - sicnu::OutputCommitter committer( m_dataManager ); ... + runOperatorTask(...)' including the '- output.autoLoad = m_addToCanvasCheck->isChecked();' consumer). m_addToCanvasCheck now has zero readers (grep: single occurrence at construction, line 141). Dead includes remain: processing_asset_resolver.h, output_committer.h, asset_index_pipeline.h, QTemporaryDir/QTemporaryFile.`
- **DLGB-9**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `检索 click -> StacClient::search (single GET, server-default limit) -> onSearchCompleted -> populateResults -> user believes the list is complete`
  - *Code context*: `StacClient::search issues a single GET {endpoint}/search with only collections/datetime/bbox query items (buildSearchUrl, stac_client.cpp:143-156); no 'limit', no paging through the STAC 'next' link or token, and the reply handler emits only root['features'] once (lines 200-202). earth-search and most STAC APIs default to ~10 items per page. populateResults then fills the table with exactly what arrived and enables Load for non-empty lists; no UI indication that more pages exist.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Highest-risk members verified. DLGB-2: createFeedback() (qgsprocessingalgorithmdialogbase.cpp:421-435) returns a parentless feedback; sicnu_algorithm_dialog.cpp:642-643 stores it in mFeedback, only ever dropped — leak per Run. DLGA-7: atmospheric_dialog.cpp only connects m_methodCombo (:55-56) and the gain/bias spinners (:102-104); m_bandCombo has NO currentIndexChanged connection, so refreshMetadata() never re-runs on band change and the spinners keep band-1 coefficients. DLGB-3: failure callback (:806-815) resets mRunState without calling postProcess; other members confirmed by evidence and surrounding code (RasterProcessingDialogBase onResult-after-accept ordering :368-396, editable-combo currentData() discard :166-169, spectral-index asset-regression, STAC first-page-only).
- **Review note**: None material. All P3.
- **Finding DLGB-2**: `'mFeedback = createFeedback();' (line 642). The vendored base createFeedback() (src/gui/processing/qgsprocessingalgorithmdialogbase.cpp:421-435) returns 'feedback.release()' — a bare, parentless object. The dialog only ever drops the pointer: 'mFeedback = nullptr;' at lines 707, 717, 726, 848; grep shows no 'delete mFeedback' anywhere. The sibling vendored batch dialog owns the same object correctly: 'mFeedback.reset( createFeedback() );' (src/gui/processing/qgsprocessingbatchalgorithmdialogbase.cpp:66).`
- **Finding DLGB-3**: `Failure callback: '[this]( const QString &err, bool ) { bool successful = false; QVariantMap results = mRunState ? mRunState->results : QVariantMap(); ... algExecuted( successful, results ); mRunState.reset(); }' — mRunState.reset() destroys the prepared algorithm instance without postProcess even when mRunState->workerStarted is true (runPrepared was entered and threw). Contract: vendored qgsprocessingalgorithm.h:437-440 'Since QGIS 3.38, postProcess() will always be called even for unsuccessful run executions, to allow the algorithm to gracefully clean up.' Reference behavior: QgsProcessingAlgRunnerTask::finished (src/core/processing/qgsprocessingalgrunnertask.cpp:83-87) calls 'mAlgorithm->postProcess( mContext, mFeedback, result )' for both success and failure. The success lambda (780-805) does call postProcess; only the failure path skips it.`

- **Finding DLGA-7**: `refreshMetadata() (lines 122-180) resolves per-band coefficients: `const int band = m_bandCombo->currentData().toInt(); ... meta.bands.value( band ); m_gainSpin->setValue( c.radianceGain ); ...` and is documented in the header as 'fills gain/bias for the selected band'. Changing 波段 afterwards leaves the spins and the '已从 %1 自动填充 gain/bias' label showing band-1 values while the combo shows another band; if the user then edits a spin (m_coefficientsModified), the band-1 value is sent as the explicit gain for the newly selected band.`
- **Finding DLGB-8**: `m_crsCombo is editable ('m_crsCombo->setEditable(true);', line 72) and setDefaultCrs explicitly supports arbitrary text ('else m_crsCombo->setCurrentText(crs);'). But defaultCrs() returns 'm_crsCombo->currentData().toString();' — for a user-typed entry no item data exists, currentData() is an invalid QVariant and toString() is empty. saveSettings() then persists 'preferences/defaultCrs' = "".`
- **Finding DLGA-9**: `filterTree only toggles `childItem->setHidden( !match )` and hides empty categories; it never clears or moves the current selection, and QTreeWidget keeps currentItem (and the OK button state from the last onTreeItemChanged) on hidden items. m_okButton is `setDefault(true)` (line 94), so pressing Enter after typing a filter accepts whatever m_selectedEpsg currently holds — possibly an item hidden by the filter.`

ISSUE #346: [P3][concurrency/lifecycle] Concurrency minors batch: shutdown() invokes CancelHooks under m_mutex (ADR 0051 violation); #207 residual detached watcher teardown race; submit-during-shutdown skips notify(); synchronous catch-up taskUpdated fires before callers store task id
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Concurrency minors batch: shutdown() invokes CancelHooks under m_mutex (ADR 0051 violation); #207 residual detached watcher teardown race; submit-during-shutdown skips notify(); synchronous catch-up taskUpdated fires before callers store task id. This issue aggregates 4 related finding(s): CONC-4, CONC-5, CONC-7, SHELLB-5. Specifically, JobEngine::shutdown() invokes CancelHooks while holding m_mutex - violates the ADR 0051 hook contract; a re-entrant hook deadlocks the shutdown thread. Specifically, #207 residual: detached ToolCallDispatcher watcher threads still outlive TaskCenter teardown - wake-then-destroy race on m_mutex/m_waitCondition, and the no-QCoreApplication fallback dereferences a destroyed DataManager via the captured committer handler. Specifically, JobEngine::submit() during shutdown creates a Cancelled record but returns inside the lock scope, skipping notify() - the listener never hears about shutdown-time submissions.

## Area
Concurrency / Job Engine / Lifecycle

## Type
concurrency/lifecycle

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/jobs/job_engine.cpp:49-90 (hooks at 64-72)` (JobEngine::shutdown)
- `src/processing/framework/tool_call_dispatcher_task_center.cpp:18-41` (ToolCallDispatcher::ToolCallDispatcher (mWatcher lambda))
- `src/jobs/job_engine.cpp:174-189` (JobEngine::submit)
- `src/app/obia/rs_obia_main_window.cpp:570-619 (m_pendingTaskId assigned only AFTER submitJob returns; onObiaTaskUpdated ignores unknown ids at 724-727); src/app/shell/gui_job_adapter.cpp:39-46,64,120-123; root cause at src/processing/framework/task_center.cpp:515-521 (in-submit catch-up -> markTask* -> flushPendingSignals -> direct emit taskUpdated)` (RsObiaMainWindow::startSegmentationTask/startHierarchyTask/startHierarchyClassifyTask; GuiJobHandle::submitJob; TaskCenter::submitJobImpl)

## User impact
- Latent shutdown deadlock / contract violation; exit hangs (join blocks forever) once any conformant hook is installed.
- Flaky use-after-free/SIGSEGV at process exit; on the else-branch, catalog mutation on a destroyed DataManager.
- Low today (TaskCenter's post-submit snapshot masks it); contract inconsistency that will surface as 'task never terminates' for any future listener-only consumer.
- Unrecoverable hang of the OBIA window or any GuiJobHandle-based dialog under a fast-terminal race; ADR 0039 task-submission robustness gap

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (CONC-4)**: Thread S (main, app exit) holds m_mutex at L50 and calls a hook at L69 that re-enters JobEngine::snapshot()/list()/cancel() - the exact usage ADR 0051 and the header comment permit. Current production hooks (georef warp: [task](){ task->cancel(); } at rs_georeferencing_session.cpp:397) do not re-enter, so today this is latent - but any ADR-0051-conformant hook deadlocks exit (the join at L96 never runs; app hangs).
- **Trigger (CONC-5)**: User quits the app while any agent/MCP tool call submitted via the default-constructed ToolCallDispatcher is still Running or just Completed: the detached watcher is inside waitForTask at teardown. Narrow windows (the 10 ms poll usually returns between shutdown() at main.cpp:510 and member destruction), which is why it manifests as the flaky teardown segfault noted in the test comment rather than a deterministic crash.
- **Trigger (CONC-7)**: Any submit racing a shutdown() window (m_shuttingDown true, i.e. between L53 and L104) - app exit with a markTask* callback on a worker re-submitting staged launches, or test code submitting during shutdownForTests-adjacent teardown.
- **Trigger (SHELLB-5)**: Submit an OBIA/GuiJobHandle job whose executor fails synchronously fast enough to win the race with the post-submit snapshot (e.g. invalid raster path with OTB present, or JobEngine shutdown)

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (CONC-4)**: shutdown() copies/moves the hooks out under the lock and invokes them after releasing m_mutex, as cancel() does.
- **Expected (CONC-5)**: #207's promise: 'Detached per-tool-call watcher threads [must not] outlive TaskCenter at exit'.
- **Expected (CONC-7)**: The terminal Cancelled record is delivered to the listener exactly like every other terminal transition (and like shutdown()'s queue-drain cancellations).
- **Expected (SHELLB-5)**: Terminal state always observed by the pending-op state machine

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (CONC-4)**: Hooks run under m_mutex; re-entrant hooks deadlock; queued-job hooks also fire although cancel() semantics say they should not.
- **Actual (CONC-5)**: Threads are still detached with a 30-min wait and a callback path that can touch a destroyed DataManager; the fix narrowed but did not close the window, and correctness now depends on test-side waiting.
- **Actual (CONC-7)**: Listener silently misses the record; 'copy' is computed and discarded. Engine-level consumers (ADR 0051 single-slot listeners other than TaskCenter's catch-up, tests) see a job id whose terminal state arrives never.
- **Actual (SHELLB-5)**: Terminal update dropped; UI stuck busy (modal dialog + WaitCursor) until app restart

## Root cause
- **CONC-4**: confirmed (latent deadlock; contract violation)
  - *Callchain*: `~JobEngine (or TaskCenter::shutdown -> JobEngine::shutdown, e.g. main.cpp:510-511) -> lock m_mutex -> kv.second.onCancel() [hook calls JobEngine::instance().snapshot(...) or any engine API] -> std::lock_guard on the same non-recursive std::mutex in the same thread -> deadlock (UB per [thread.mutex.requirements])`
  - *Code context*: `  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock( m_mutex );   // L50
    ...
    for ( auto &kv : m_jobBodies ) {
      if ( kv.second.onCancel ) {
        try { kv.second.onCancel(); } catch ( ... ) {}   // L64-72: INSIDE the lock
      }
    }
    ... queue drain ...
  }                                                 // L90 lock released
job_engine.h L54-59 documents the opposite contract: 'Hooks are invoked WITHOUT the engine lock held, so they may re-enter JobEngine (e.g. snapshot()) without deadlocking', and ADR 0051 decision 4 (docs/adr/0051 L28-30) fixed exactly this deadlock for cancel() ('JobEngine::cancel invokes the CancelHook after releasing m_mutex', implemented at job_engine.cpp:283-295). shutdown() still runs hooks under the lock. shutdown() additionally fires hooks for QUEUED bodies, which cancel() deliberately does not (it erases the body instead, job_engine.cpp:250).`
- **CONC-5**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `MCP/agent submit -> mWatcher -> detached std::thread -> TaskCenter::instance().waitForTask (poll up to 30 min) || main(): app->exec returns -> window.reset() [DataManager dies] -> TaskCenter::shutdown() [wakeAll] -> delete app -> return main -> ~TaskCenter destroys m_mutex/m_waitCondition || watcher thread re-locks m_mutex / invokes committerHandler`
  - *Code context*: `  std::thread( [taskId, cb, committerHandler, bridge]() mutable {
      const auto info = sicnu::TaskCenter::instance().waitForTask( taskId );   // default 30-min wait
      if ( bridge && QCoreApplication::instance() ) { ... QueuedConnection ... }
      else if ( cb ) {
          const Json::Value payload = buildTaskResultPayload( info, committerHandler );  // committerHandler captured dataManager RAW (tool_call_dispatcher.cpp:62-96); if info.status==Completed && outputLayerPath non-empty it runs OutputCommitter(dataManager)->registerSource
          cb( payload );
      }
  } ).detach();
The #207 fix (commit 5116f95a66) only swapped BlockingQueuedConnection for QueuedConnection plus the instance() guard and added m_isShuttingDown to waitForTask. Residuals: (a) ~TaskCenter (static destruction after main returns) calls shutdown() -> wakeAll under m_mutex (task_center.cpp:55-58) and then destroys m_mutex/m_waitCondition/m_tasks while the detached thread is returning from QWaitCondition::wait (which must RE-LOCK m_mutex) or re-checking m_isShuttingDown - a destroy-vs-use race on the mutex and members. (b) after `delete app` (main.cpp:514) QCoreApplication::instance() is null, so the else-branch runs buildTaskResultPayload with committerHandler whose captured DataManager* was destroyed with window.reset() (main.cpp:508) -> registerSource on a dead object. tests/test_tool_call_dispatcher.cpp:783-799 documents the teardown segfault and works around it by polling for terminal state instead of fixing the lifetime ('process static teardown would then tear down TaskCenter under the thread (segfault) Wait for a terminal state so the detached thread is done before main() returns').`
- **CONC-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Thread T: TaskCenter::flushPendingLaunches -> JobEngine::submit while thread S is inside JobEngine::shutdown (m_shuttingDown true) -> record stored Cancelled -> return without notify -> engine-level listeners observe nothing; TaskCenter's catch-up snapshot repairs its own bookkeeping only`
  - *Code context*: `  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock( m_mutex );
  if ( m_shuttingDown ) {
      ...
      rec.state = JobState::Cancelled;
      ...
      m_jobs.emplace( id, rec );
      copy = rec;          // dead store
      return id;           // L189: returns INSIDE the lock scope; the tail's notify(copy) (L218) is never reached
  }
Contrast shutdown()'s queue drain, which deliberately collects cancelledRecords and notifies after releasing the lock (L93-94), and ADR 0051's premise that the single listener is the authoritative forwarding path. TaskCenter only survives this because submitJobImpl/flushPendingLaunches re-snapshot the job after submit (task_center.cpp:519-520, 804-805) and the catch-up marks the task Canceled.`
- **SHELLB-5**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `OBIA Segment click -> startSegmentationTask -> TaskCenter::submitJobImpl -> (fast-fail executor) -> onJobRecord -> markTaskFailed -> flushPendingSignals -> emit taskUpdated -> onObiaTaskUpdated returns early -> submitJob returns -> m_pendingTaskId = <already-terminal id> -> stuck`
  - *Code context*: `submitJobImpl ends with `if ( const auto record = JobEngine::instance().snapshot( jobId ) ) onJobRecord( *record );` — if the worker reached a terminal state before submit returned, markTaskCompleted/Failed/Canceled runs on the calling (GUI) thread inside submitJob and flushPendingSignals() emits taskUpdated with a DIRECT connection to RsObiaMainWindow::onObiaTaskUpdated, which early-returns because `info.taskId != m_pendingTaskId` (m_pendingTaskId is still -1; it is assigned at line 614 only after submitJob returns). The task is already terminal so no further signal ever arrives, yet m_pendingTaskId/m_pendingOp/progress dialog/WaitCursor were already set (lines 565-568) — isBusy() stays true forever, the modal QProgressDialog never closes, QGuiApplication::restoreOverrideCursor is never called, and every subsequent action shows "An OBIA task is already running". Same shape in GuiJobHandle: m_taskId is assigned after m_taskCenter->submitJob(...) returns; a synchronous terminal update is dropped and the handle stays isRunning()==true, permanently returning -1 for later submissions. Window probability is low (worker must finish inside the submit->snapshot window, e.g. an executor that throws immediately or engine shutdown), but the failure mode is unrecoverable without closing the window/app.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: CONC-4 verified: shutdown() invokes every onCancel hook INSIDE the lock_guard scope (job_engine.cpp:49-90, hooks at :64-72) while cancel() pops the hook then invokes it OUTSIDE the lock (:283-299) per the documented ADR 0051 contract, and job_engine.h:54-59 explicitly promises hooks run without the lock. Deadlock is stimulus-dependent (production georef hook [task](){task->cancel();} rs_georeferencing_session.cpp:397 does not currently re-enter JobEngine), so today it is latent — kept as a real contract violation, not dropped, because shutdown() is reachable on every app exit and the violation is instantiated code. CONC-5 verified (detached std::thread watcher in tool_call_dispatcher_task_center.cpp:18-41 waiting without bound + no-QCoreApplication fallback touching committerHandler). CONC-7 verified (submit-during-shutdown `return id` inside lock, dead `copy` store).
- **Review note**: Label CONC-4 as latent-contract-violation rather than live deadlock in the file; severity P3 stands.
- **Finding CONC-4**: `  {
    std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock( m_mutex );   // L50
    ...
    for ( auto &kv : m_jobBodies ) {
      if ( kv.second.onCancel ) {
        try { kv.second.onCancel(); } catch ( ... ) {}   // L64-72: INSIDE the lock
      }
    }
    ... queue drain ...
  }                                                 // L90 lock released

- **Finding CONC-5**: `  std::thread( [taskId, cb, committerHandler, bridge]() mutable {

  } ).detach();
The #207 fix (commit 5116f95a66) only swapped BlockingQueuedConnection for QueuedConnection plus the instance() guard and added m_isShuttingDown to waitForTask. Residuals: (a) ~TaskCenter (static destruction after main returns) calls shutdown() -> wakeAll under m_mutex (task_center.cpp:55-58) and then destroys m_mutex/m_waitCondition/m_tasks while the detached thread is returning from QWaitCondition::wait (which must RE-LOCK m_mutex) or re-checking m_isShuttingDown - a destroy-vs-use race on the mutex and members. - **Finding CONC-7**: `  std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock( m_mutex );
  if ( m_shuttingDown ) {
      ...
      rec.state = JobState::Cancelled;
      ...
      m_jobs.emplace( id, rec );
      copy = rec;          // dead store
      return id;           // L189: returns INSIDE the lock scope; the tail's notify(copy) (L218) is never reached
  }
Contrast shutdown()'s queue drain, which deliberately collects cancelledRecords and notifies after releasing the lock (L93-94), and ADR 0051's premise that the single listener is the authoritative forwarding path. TaskCenter only survives this because submitJobImpl/flushPendingLaunches re-snapshot the job after submit (task_center.cpp:519-520, 804-805) and the catch-up marks the task Canceled.`
- **Finding SHELLB-5**: `submitJobImpl ends with `if ( const auto record = JobEngine::instance().snapshot( jobId ) ) onJobRecord( *record );` — if the worker reached a terminal state before submit returned, markTaskCompleted/Failed/Canceled runs on the calling (GUI) thread inside submitJob and flushPendingSignals() emits taskUpdated with a DIRECT connection to RsObiaMainWindow::onObiaTaskUpdated, which early-returns because `info.taskId != m_pendingTaskId` (m_pendingTaskId is still -1; it is assigned at line 614 only after submitJob returns). Same shape in GuiJobHandle: m_taskId is assigned after m_taskCenter->submitJob(...) returns; a synchronous terminal update is dropped and the handle stays isRunning()==true, permanently returning -1 for later submissions. Window probability is low (worker must finish inside the submit->snapshot window, e.g. an executor that throws immediately or engine shutdown), but the failure mode is unrecoverable without closing the window/app.`

## Suggested fix
- Ensure proper synchronization, atomic state transitions, clean thread termination, and avoidance of use-after-free or race conditions on worker/GUI threads.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (CONC-4, CONC-5, CONC-7, SHELLB-5).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.

- Finding SHELLB-5: #209 (RSS gate starvation), #220 (destructor re-entrancy), #206 (zombie launches) are different defects; the catch-up comment in task_center.cpp acknowledges the terminal-before-mapping case but not the consumer-side id-assignment race.

ISSUE #348: [P3][agent/mcp] Agent-surface minors: synchronous bandStatistics on GUI thread; CLI lacks signal-based cancellation and engine shutdown
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Agent-surface minors: synchronous bandStatistics on GUI thread; CLI lacks signal-based cancellation and engine shutdown. This issue aggregates 2 related finding(s): AGMCP-12, AGMCP-15. Specifically, Synchronous raster bandStatistics on the GUI thread in agent display tools and per-message WorkspaceSnapshot — residual instance of the #218 family. Specifically, CLI has no signal-based cancellation and never shuts down TaskCenter/JobEngine — dirty exit while worker threads run.

## Area
Agent Interface / Model Context Protocol (MCP)

## Type
agent/mcp

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/agent/raster_display_service.cpp:32-57 (also src/agent/workspace_snapshot.cpp:86-99)` (createBandEnhancement + WorkspaceSnapshot::captureActiveRaster)
- `src/cli/main_cli.cpp:100-118` (main (cli) — no SIGINT/SIGTERM handling, no shutdown calls)

## User impact
- Canvas/UI freezes during copilot display operations on big scenes; same class as closed #218
- Corrupted partial outputs and nondeterministic exit crashes; 'cancel via signal' focus item unimplemented

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (AGMCP-12)**: Copilot sets a band composite on a large uncached/remote raster layer
- **Trigger (AGMCP-15)**: 30-minute pipeline timeout, failed step with parallel siblings, or SIGINT during a run

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (AGMCP-12)**: Stats computed off the GUI thread or cached (as #218 did for the ribbon/histogram paths)
- **Expected (AGMCP-15)**: Signal handler maps SIGINT/SIGTERM to TaskCenter cancellation; explicit shutdown before exit

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (AGMCP-12)**: Blocking sample scan(s) on the GUI thread per agent call and per chat message
- **Actual (AGMCP-15)**: Abrupt process death or undefined behavior at static-destruction with running threads (crash/hang at exit)

## Root cause
- **AGMCP-12**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `LLM raster:set_band_composite -> RasterDisplayService::setBandComposite (GUI thread) -> createBandEnhancement -> bandStatistics -> provider scan`
  - *Code context*: `createBandEnhancement calls provider->bandStatistics(band, Min|Max) synchronously inside executeOnGuiThread lambdas — raster:set_band_composite triggers up to 3 scans (R/G/B), raster:reset_display up to 3, and sendPrompt's WorkspaceSnapshot::capture does another (statsBand) on every user message. Neither file was touched by the #218 fix commit 8ed2e0e99c (verified via git log).`
- **AGMCP-15**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `timeout/failure -> runFromFile returns -> main returns 1 -> static destruction of TaskCenter while JobEngine workers execute operators`
  - *Code context*: `The only TaskCenter::shutdown()/JobEngine::shutdown() call site in the tree is src/app/main.cpp:510-511 (GUI). After a failed or timed-out pipeline (rs_pipeline_runner.cpp:482-487 returns while sibling tasks may still be running) or after Ctrl+C (default disposition kills the process mid-operator), main returns and static singletons destruct with live worker threads. There is also no way to request TaskCenter cancellation of a running CLI pipeline at all.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: AGMCP-12 verified: createBandEnhancement (raster_display_service.cpp:32-57) calls provider->bandStatistics(Band Min|Max) synchronously inside executeOnGuiThread lambdas — up to 3 full-band scans per display tool invocation of the #218 family on the GUI thread. AGMCP-15 verified: the only TaskCenter/JobEngine shutdown() call sites are src/app/main.cpp:510-511; src/cli/main_cli.cpp contains none. Correction/partial mitigation: JobEngine is a process-local singleton whose destructor calls shutdown() (joining workers) at static destruction, so the exit is not fully dirty — the harmful gap is mid-run shutdown absence (failed/timed-out pipeline leaves sibling tasks running while the CLI proceeds).
- **Review note**: Fold the singleton-destructor mitigation into the AGMCP-15 write-up; severity unchanged.
- **Finding AGMCP-12**: `createBandEnhancement calls provider->bandStatistics(band, Min|Max) synchronously inside executeOnGuiThread lambdas — raster:set_band_composite triggers up to 3 scans (R/G/B), raster:reset_display up to 3, and sendPrompt's WorkspaceSnapshot::capture does another (statsBand) on every user message. Neither file was touched by the #218 fix commit 8ed2e0e99c (verified via git log).`
- **Finding AGMCP-15**: `The only TaskCenter::shutdown()/JobEngine::shutdown() call site in the tree is src/app/main.cpp:510-511 (GUI). After a failed or timed-out pipeline (rs_pipeline_runner.cpp:482-487 returns while sibling tasks may still be running) or after Ctrl+C (default disposition kills the process mid-operator), main returns and static singletons destruct with live worker threads. There is also no way to request TaskCenter cancellation of a running CLI pipeline at all.`

## Suggested fix
- Comply with MCP protocol specifications, raise proper JSON-RPC errors, and handle lifecycle/path policies securely.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (AGMCP-12, AGMCP-15).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- AGMCP-12 is explicitly a residual of #218; no prior CLI-shutdown issue.
- Finding AGMCP-12: #218 fixed widget paths only; agent-service paths are uncovered instances — partial overlap flagged
- Finding AGMCP-15: #177/#178 cover GUI teardown; #105 closed 'CLI via TaskCenter' without exit/cancel handling — new CLI-surface variant

ISSUE #350: [P3][data/crs-nodata-metadata] rs: operator minors batch 2: OBIA segment means include NoData; recode/majority silent fallbacks; BandMath materializes all bands + per-pixel heap alloc; partial outputs left on failure; schema-vs-result drift
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
rs: operator minors batch 2: OBIA segment means include NoData; recode/majority silent fallbacks; BandMath materializes all bands + per-pixel heap alloc; partial outputs left on failure; schema-vs-result drift. This issue aggregates 6 related finding(s): OPS-7, OPS-9, OPS-10, RSALGO-11, OPS-11, OPS-12. Specifically, rs:obia_classify per-segment mean features accumulate NoData/non-finite band values — sentinel pixels poison segment means. Specifically, rs:recode silently returns an identity copy for malformed recode_map; rs:majority_filter silently coerces invalid kernel to 3. Specifically, BandMath::processFile materializes every input band even when the expression references only a few — O(W*H*B) memory for an O(W*H*refs) problem.

## Area
Data Model / Raster Grids / NoData & CRS

## Type
data/crs-nodata-metadata

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/operators/rs/rs_obia_classify_operator.cpp:239-264` (RsObiaClassifyOperator::run (feature accumulation))
- `src/operators/rs/rs_recode_operator.cpp:90-119, 199-206; src/operators/rs/rs_majority_filter_operator.cpp:113-116` (parseRecodeMap / RsRecodeOperator::run / RsMajorityFilterOperator::run)
- `src/processing/algorithms/band_math.cpp:608-651` (BandMath::processFile)
- `src/processing/algorithms/band_math.cpp:171-178, 623-641` (BandMath::FunctionCallNode::eval / BandMath::processFile)
- `src/operators/rs/rs_apply_mask_operator.cpp:285-292 (create) and 355-372 (mid-stream throws with no cleanup); src/processing/algorithms/radiometric_calibration.cpp:513-569 (create then per-band stream, bare `return false` on tile failure despite the 498-499 comment claiming 'deterministic failures never leave a partial output'); rs_continuum_removal_operator.cpp:142-183; rs_spectral_resample_operator.cpp:242-286; gdal_orthorectification_operator.cpp:212-223` (RsApplyMaskOperator::run / RadiometricCalibration::processFile / RsContinuumRemovalOperator::run / GdalOrthorectificationOperator::run)
- `src/operators/rs/rs_band_math_operator.cpp:(a) 27-32 vs 100-103; (b) src/operators/rs/rs_image_fusion_operator.cpp:42-43 vs 132-135 and rs_fusion_aliases.cpp:32-35 vs 120-123; (c) src/operators/otb/otb_compute_images_statistics_operator.cpp:35,89-91; (d) src/operators/rs/rs_spectral_unmixing_operator.cpp:58-73; (e) src/operators/opencv/opencv_utils.cpp:14-41 vs 56-70 consumed by rs_inference_operator.cpp:116-128; (f) src/operators/gdal/gdal_polygonize_operator.cpp:205-215; (g) src/operators/rs/rs_mosaic_operator.cpp:203-208 and src/data/raster_grid_compat.cpp (CRS WKT compare)` (RsBandMathOperator::schema/run, RsImageFusionOperator, OtbComputeImagesStatisticsOperator, parseBands(local), readRasterBandToMat, GdalPolygonizeOperator, RsMosaicOperator)

## User impact
- Wrong object features -> wrong classification, or confusing training failure; inconsistent with the project's own NoData conventions fixed for the sibling operators.
- Agents believe the recode succeeded; user gets an output that is byte-different but semantically identical, with no error to self-correct on.
- Memory blowups and RSS-gate starvation for a computation that should be near-constant in band count; declared estimate materially wrong.
- 2-5x slower kernel evaluation on large scenes and multi-GB memory ceiling on hyperspectral inputs; GUI/pipeline runs report FullRaster memory policy but consume all bands.
- Downstream steps and users consume corrupt rasters; the 227 fix pattern was not propagated to the rs: family.
- Agent tool-calling reliability: agents reference output keys that never exist, pass booleans where MB are expected, and get confusing late errors instead of typed validation.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (OPS-7)**: rs:obia_classify on a multi-band raster where any band other than band 1 contains NoData/sentinel pixels overlapping labeled segments.
- **Trigger (OPS-9)**: Agent passes recode_map='{1:2}' (invalid JSON — unquoted keys) or {"1":"2"} (string values, skipped by asInt+catch), or majority_filter kernel=4.
- **Trigger (OPS-10)**: rs:band_math on any multi-band raster, worst on hyperspectral stacks (100-250 bands) where a 2-band formula still loads the full cube.
- **Trigger (RSALGO-11)**: Any band-math expression containing a function (sqrt/min/max/pow/...) on a large or multi-band raster
- **Trigger (OPS-11)**: Any mid-stream read/write failure or cancellation-unfriendly error (bad band, disk full, unit-specific coefficient missing) after the output create() succeeds.
- **Trigger (OPS-12)**: Agents reading the schema to predict result keys; otb ram usage; unmixing with bad bands; infer with bands param on a NoData raster; polygonize overwrite when rename fails; cross-producer mosaics.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (OPS-7)**: Per-band NoData/NaN filtering (skip the pixel for the mean, or the segment if contaminated) as done in segment_stats/kmeans.
- **Expected (OPS-9)**: InvalidParameter errors, consistent with the framework's typed validation used everywhere else.
- **Expected (OPS-10)**: Pre-parse the expression (Parser is a local class) to collect band refs and read only those bands; memory O(W*H*(refs+1)).
- **Expected (RSALGO-11)**: Fixed-size stack/small-buffer for args (max 2 supported); tiled streaming like processPcaFile
- **Expected (OPS-11)**: RAII cleaner removing the partial output when the run did not commit (the pattern already present in mosaic/change-streaming/runGdalWarp).
- **Expected (OPS-12)**: Schema outputs match produced keys; otb ram numeric in MB; framework parseBands reused; consistent NoData conversion; rename checked; CRS compared semantically (or documented as strict).

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (OPS-7)**: -9999 sentinels shift segment means arbitrarily (silent bias); NaN bands make the whole feature NaN and SVM fit fails with the opaque 'SVM training failed'.
- **Actual (OPS-9)**: recode writes an unmodified copy labeled success (and MCP reports completion); majority filter runs with a silently changed kernel.
- **Actual (OPS-10)**: O(W*H*B) resident floats; large cubes can OOM the JobEngine worker or trip the RSS admission gate despite a trivial formula.
- **Actual (RSALGO-11)**: ~1 malloc per pixel per function node + O(bands*W*H) memory
- **Actual (OPS-11)**: Truncated GeoTIFFs remain at the requested output path while the operator reports failure.
- **Actual (OPS-12)**: Drift between declared and actual result keys and several silent-default/validation gaps as listed.

## Root cause
- **OPS-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `MCP/CLI execute_algorithm -> RsOperatorAdapter::execute -> RsObiaClassifyOperator::run -> trainX/feats -> RsClassifierBackendFactory SVM/Bayes fit + predict.`
  - *Code context*: `The accumulation loop only skips sid==0 (`if (sid == 0) continue;` line 246-247) and then does `sum[sid][f] += bandData[f][pix]` for every feature band with no isfinite/NoData check. NoData was resolved from band 1 only for the segmentation step (200-203); a pixel valid in band 1 but NoData in band 2..N (very common in stacked products with per-band masks) contributes its sentinel (-9999) or NaN to every overlapping segment's mean. Contrast rs:segment_stats (filters NaN/inf/declared NoData per band, rs_segment_stats_operator.cpp:146-155) and rs:kmeans (isPixelValid, rs_kmeans_operator.cpp:183-193).`
- **OPS-9**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `MCP/CLI execute_algorithm -> RsOperatorAdapter::execute -> operator run().`
  - *Code context*: `parseRecodeMap: when recode_map is a string, JSON parsing is attempted (94-100) and on failure mapValue stays a string; the object branch skips non-int keys/values inside catch(...) (104-117). An empty resulting map takes the identity path `outLabels = labels;` (204-206) and the operator writes a full '_recode.tif' and returns success. Similarly majority_filter: `if (kernel < 3 || kernel % 2 == 0) kernel = 3;` silently rewrites an invalid user kernel instead of throwing (every other operator throws InvalidParameter for bad kernels, e.g. opencv filters).`
- **OPS-10**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `MCP/CLI execute_algorithm -> RsOperatorAdapter::execute -> RsBandMathOperator::run -> BandMath::processFile.`
  - *Code context*: ``for (int i = 1; i <= bandCount; ++i) { std::vector<float> buffer(pixelCount); ... bands[i] = std::move(buffer); }` — all bandCount bands are read before evaluate() parses the expression and discovers the referenced bands (collectRefs at 588-595 runs only inside evaluate). A '(b1-b2)/(b1+b2)' expression on a 224-band 1 MP hyperspectral cube holds 224 x 4 MB ≈ 900 MB plus output, versus ~12 MB needed; the operator's executionEstimate advertises '4 input bands + 1 output = ~20 MiB' (rs_band_math_operator.cpp:52-61), understating the real working set ~45x.`
- **RSALGO-11**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `rs:band_math (rs_band_math_operator.cpp:89) -> BandMath::processFile -> evaluate (per-pixel AST eval)`
  - *Code context*: `FunctionCallNode::eval body: `std::vector<float> vals; vals.reserve(args.size()); for (const auto &arg : args) vals.push_back(arg->eval(bands, pixel)); return callFunction(name, vals);` - one heap allocation + free per pixel per function node (reserve avoids growth but not the allocation). An expression like sqrt((b1-b2)/(b1+b2)) on a 100MP raster = 100M malloc/free pairs through the expression evaluator. processFile additionally reads every band fully: `for (i=1..bandCount) { std::vector<float> buffer(pixelCount); readBandData(...); }` - 224-band 1MP hyperspectral = ~900 MB resident vs the O(tile) streaming used by the calibration/PCA paths.`
- **OPS-11**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `MCP/CLI execute_algorithm -> RsOperatorAdapter::execute -> operator run(); pipeline steps may then consume $stepId.output pointing at the truncated file, and re-runs see a pre-existing output.`
  - *Code context*: `rs:mosaic (OutputFileCleaner, rs_mosaic_operator.cpp:44-59) and the change kernel (DatasetFileGuard, rs_change_streaming.cpp:28-46) and runGdalWarp (gdal_operator_utils.cpp:165-188) all remove the partial output on failure — but the operators above create the output dataset up front and throw/return-false mid-stream with the truncated file still on disk. Radiometric calibration's comment at 498-499 explicitly promises no partial outputs, yet toBrightnessTemperature returning false (missing K1/K2 for the requested unit) fails on the first tile AFTER the output file was created.`
- **OPS-12**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `MCP/CLI execute_algorithm -> RsOperatorAdapter -> each operator's schema() (agent tool JSON + catalog) vs run() results.`
  - *Code context*: `(a) band_math schema declares outputs width/height; run() returns only output+expression (header doc promises width/height too). (b) image_fusion + all 5 fusion aliases declare outputs['bands'] but never emit it. (c) otb compute_images_statistics declares `ram` as a boolean ('Enable RAM estimation mode') and passes literal '-ram 256' — OTB's -ram is available RAM in MB with default 256, so the flag is a no-op and agents cannot set a real value. (d) spectral_unmixing has a private parseBands with no 1..bandCount range or type validation (unlike framework params::parseBands) — out-of-range bands fail later as a confusing GDAL read error, non-numeric entries surface as jsoncpp logic_error. (e) readRasterBandToMat does not convert NoData->NaN while readRasterBandsToMats does, so rs:infer model input changes NoData handling depending on whether `bands` was passed. (f) polygonize's final publish does QFile::rename without checking the result after already deleting the user's previous output — a failed rename destroys the old result and reports success. (g) mosaic and compareGrids compare CRS by raw WKT string equality: the same CRS with different WKT spellings (producer/GDAL-version differences) is falsely rejected as 'CRS mismatch'.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: High-risk members verified. OPS-7: per-segment mean accumulation in rs_obia_classify_operator.cpp:239-264 skips only sid==0; adds every feature-band value with no isfinite/NoData test (NoData resolved from band 1 only), so sentinels/NaN in other bands poison segment means. RSALGO-11/OPS-10: band_math.cpp FunctionCallNode::eval heap-allocates a std::vector per pixel per function call (:171-178) and processFile materializes ALL bands before parsing the expression (:608-651).
- **Review note**: None. All P3.
- **Finding OPS-7**: `The accumulation loop only skips sid==0 (`if (sid == 0) continue;` line 246-247) and then does `sum[sid][f] += bandData[f][pix]` for every feature band with no isfinite/NoData check. NoData was resolved from band 1 only for the segmentation step (200-203); a pixel valid in band 1 but NoData in band 2..N (very common in stacked products with per-band masks) contributes its sentinel (-9999) or NaN to every overlapping segment's mean. Contrast rs:segment_stats (filters NaN/inf/declared NoData per band, rs_segment_stats_operator.cpp:146-155) and rs:kmeans (isPixelValid, rs_kmeans_operator.cpp:183-193).`
- **Finding OPS-9**: `parseRecodeMap: when recode_map is a string, JSON parsing is attempted (94-100) and on failure mapValue stays a string; the object branch skips non-int keys/values inside catch(...) (104-117). An empty resulting map takes the identity path `outLabels = labels;` (204-206) and the operator writes a full '_recode.tif' and returns success. Similarly majority_filter: `if (kernel < 3 || kernel % 2 == 0) kernel = 3;` silently rewrites an invalid user kernel instead of throwing (every other operator throws InvalidParameter for bad kernels, e.g. opencv filters).`
- **Finding OPS-10**: ``for (int i = 1; i <= bandCount; ++i) { std::vector<float> buffer(pixelCount); ... bands[i] = std::move(buffer); }` — all bandCount bands are read before evaluate() parses the expression and discovers the referenced bands (collectRefs at 588-595 runs only inside evaluate). A '(b1-b2)/(b1+b2)' expression on a 224-band 1 MP hyperspectral cube holds 224 x 4 MB ≈ 900 MB plus output, versus ~12 MB needed; the operator's executionEstimate advertises '4 input bands + 1 output = ~20 MiB' (rs_band_math_operator.cpp:52-61), understating the real working set ~45x.`
- **Finding RSALGO-11**: `FunctionCallNode::eval body: `std::vector<float> vals; vals.reserve(args.size()); for (const auto &arg : args) vals.push_back(arg->eval(bands, pixel)); return callFunction(name, vals);` - one heap allocation + free per pixel per function node (reserve avoids growth but not the allocation). - **Finding OPS-11**: `rs:mosaic (OutputFileCleaner, rs_mosaic_operator.cpp:44-59) and the change kernel (DatasetFileGuard, rs_change_streaming.cpp:28-46) and runGdalWarp (gdal_operator_utils.cpp:165-188) all remove the partial output on failure — but the operators above create the output dataset up front and throw/return-false mid-stream with the truncated file still on disk. Radiometric calibration's comment at 498-499 explicitly promises no partial outputs, yet toBrightnessTemperature returning false (missing K1/K2 for the requested unit) fails on the first tile AFTER the output file was created.`
- **Finding OPS-12**: `(a) band_math schema declares outputs width/height; run() returns only output+expression (header doc promises width/height too). (b) image_fusion + all 5 fusion aliases declare outputs['bands'] but never emit it. (c) otb compute_images_statistics declares `ram` as a boolean ('Enable RAM estimation mode') and passes literal '-ram 256' — OTB's -ram is available RAM in MB with default 256, so the flag is a no-op and agents cannot set a real value. (d) spectral_unmixing has a private parseBands with no 1..bandCount range or type validation (unlike framework params::parseBands) — out-of-range bands fail later as a confusing GDAL read error, non-numeric entries surface as jsoncpp logic_error. (f) polygonize's final publish does QFile::rename without checking the result after already deleting the user's previous output — a failed rename destroys the old result and reports success. (g) mosaic and compareGrids compare CRS by raw WKT string equality: the same CRS with different WKT spellings (producer/GDAL-version differences) is falsely rejected as 'CRS mismatch'.`

## Suggested fix
- Validate grid compatibility rigorously, propagate NoData masks across all processing steps, and preserve CRS/metadata on output datasets.

## Acceptance criteria

ISSUE #352: [P3][processing/provider] OTB/native provider minors batch 2: auto-discovery -in/-out skeleton; blocking QMessageBox in headless load; bilateral mislabel; extension-append existence check; merged-channel stderr; hardcoded ':' PATH; distance-matrix output type ignored; symdiff/intersection attribute loss; extract-by-attribute string compares
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
OTB/native provider minors batch 2: auto-discovery -in/-out skeleton; blocking QMessageBox in headless load; bilateral mislabel; extension-append existence check; merged-channel stderr; hardcoded ':' PATH; distance-matrix output type ignored; symdiff/intersection attribute loss; extract-by-attribute string compares. This issue aggregates 9 related finding(s): PROVO-6, PROVO-7, PROVO-8, PROVO-9, PROVO-10, PROVO-11, PROVO-12, PROVO-13, PROVO-14. Specifically, Auto-discovered OTB applications are registered with a fixed '-in {INPUT} -out {OUTPUT}' argument skeleton most OTB apps do not accept - discovered tools mostly cannot run and EXTRA cannot remove the invented keys. Specifically, GenericCliProvider::loadToolsFromDirectory pops a blocking QMessageBox for malformed tool JSON during provider loading - headless/worker startup can hang forever. Specifically, Shipped custom OTB JSON tools mislabeled: otb_bilateral_filter.json runs a Gaussian smoothing while advertising bilateral/edge-preserving behavior - #240's label/behavior cleanup stopped at the median file.

## Area
Processing Framework / GDAL & OTB Providers

## Type
processing/provider

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/processing/providers/otb_tools/provider.cpp:100-105 (registration); src/processing/tools/cli_tool_discovery.cpp:329-354 (config builder)` (OtbToolsProvider::loadAlgorithms / CliToolDiscovery::makeOtbDiscoveredConfig)
- `src/processing/providers/generic_cli/provider.cpp:69-81` (GenericCliProvider::loadToolsFromDirectory)
- `data/tools/custom/otb_bilateral_filter.json:2-16,44-46 (id/name/command/args/help); data/tools/custom/otb_median_filter.json (filename vs mean content)` (JSON tool definition vs otbcli_Smoothing behavior)
- `src/processing/providers/otb_tools/otb_tool_wrapper.cpp:109-133` (OtbToolWrapper::processAlgorithm (result collection))
- `src/processing/providers/otb_tools/algorithms/otb_pixel_info.cpp:50-77; otb_read_image_info.cpp:44-71` (OtbPixelInfoAlgorithm::processAlgorithm / OtbReadImageInfoAlgorithm::processAlgorithm)
- `src/processing/providers/otb_tools/otb_tool_wrapper.cpp:178-179; src/processing/providers/generic_cli/generic_cli_algorithm.cpp:361-362` (OtbToolWrapper::runOtbApplication / GenericCliAlgorithm::processAlgorithm)
- `src/processing/providers/qgis_algorithms/algorithms/vector/vector_distance_matrix.cpp:39-48, 100-109, 188-190` (VectorDistanceMatrixAlgorithm::initAlgorithm/processAlgorithm)
- `src/processing/providers/qgis_algorithms/algorithms/vector/vector_symmetrical_difference.cpp:119-126; native_intersection.h:56-58,92-98` (VectorSymmetricalDifferenceAlgorithm::processAlgorithm / QgsIntersectionAlgorithm::processAlgorithm)
- `src/processing/providers/qgis_algorithms/algorithms/native/native_extract_by_attribute.h:41-43,76-77` (QgsExtractByAttributeAlgorithm::processAlgorithm)

## User impact
- catalog promises tools that always error; noisy UX, duplicated surface with the 13 already-broken handcrafted wrappers
- app/worker startup hang from a bad user config file
- user-selected filtering semantics silently wrong; same drift class #240 closed against, incompletely swept
- false failures and lost outputs on a legal user input; TEMPORARY_OUTPUT is unaffected (extension generated by QGIS)
- degraded diagnostics and env divergence in exactly the failure path users need to read
- OTB toolchain unusable on Windows packaged builds
- user-visible schema drift; downstream consumers asking for the matrix shape get the wrong layout
- output layers lose the attribute data users expect from equivalently-named QGIS algorithms
- empty or wrong selections for numeric fields despite the QGIS-native naming

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (PROVO-6)**: open any auto-discovered OTB tool (everything not in the 30 handcrafted apps) in a build with the OTB bundle present and run it
- **Trigger (PROVO-7)**: user drops a syntactically invalid .json into the custom tools dir, then starts the app or headless/MCP worker
- **Trigger (PROVO-8)**: run the shipped 'OTB 双边滤波' (bilateral) tool
- **Trigger (PROVO-9)**: user types an output path without extension in the raster/vector destination widget of any OTB tool
- **Trigger (PROVO-10)**: any failing otbcli_PixelValue/ReadImageInfo invocation (e.g. invalid coordinates or unreadable raster)
- **Trigger (PROVO-11)**: any OTB or generic otbcli_* tool on a Windows build with a bundled OTB
- **Trigger (PROVO-12)**: choose 'Standard (N x K x M)' style matrix output
- **Trigger (PROVO-13)**: run Symmetrical Difference (any input) or Intersection (any input) and inspect the attribute table
- **Trigger (PROVO-14)**: filter a numeric (int/float/bool) field or use any non-equality operator expectation

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (PROVO-6)**: discovered tools either expose generic passthrough args or declare that only -in/-out-style apps are usable
- **Expected (PROVO-7)**: log the error (QgsMessageLog/SICNU_LOG) and skip, as the rest of the processing stack does
- **Expected (PROVO-8)**: label/id describe the actual behavior (Gaussian) or the tool is removed/renamed
- **Expected (PROVO-9)**: fall back to checking/returning path+'.tif'/'.shp' (or normalize the destination before running)
- **Expected (PROVO-10)**: error text includes the OTB stderr/stdout tail and env parity with the shared runner
- **Expected (PROVO-11)**: QDir::listSeparator() (or platform branch)
- **Expected (PROVO-12)**: wide/standard matrix format as advertised (QGIS DistanceMatrix semantics)
- **Expected (PROVO-13)**: attributes preserved/carried through per QGIS-native semantics
- **Expected (PROVO-14)**: typed comparison with operator selection (parameterAsEnum of operators, numeric compare via QVariant::toDouble)

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (PROVO-6)**: ~70 toolbox entries that structurally cannot execute for apps with different key names
- **Actual (PROVO-7)**: startup blocks on a modal dialog with no parent; in offscreen/headless platforms there is no one to dismiss it
- **Actual (PROVO-8)**: Gaussian smoothing delivered under a bilateral contract
- **Actual (PROVO-9)**: 'Tool reported success but output file is missing: <path>' exception and no output registered
- **Actual (PROVO-10)**: 'OTB application failed with exit code 1: ' - empty cause; user cannot diagnose
- **Actual (PROVO-11)**: PATH corrupted; OTB subprocess launch fails
- **Actual (PROVO-12)**: linear pair list without rank
- **Actual (PROVO-13)**: symdiff: all attributes empty; intersection: overlay columns absent
- **Actual (PROVO-14)**: string equality only; numeric formats and operators unsupported; NULL=='' edge match

## Root cause
- **PROVO-6**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `OtbToolsProvider::loadAlgorithms -> discoverOtbApplicationNames -> GenericCliAlgorithm(config) -> processAlgorithm -> buildArgs emits '-in <path> -out <path>' -> otbcli rejects`
  - *Code context*: `makeOtbDiscoveredConfig always inserts args ["-in","{INPUT}","-out","{OUTPUT}"] plus INPUT/OUTPUT parameter definitions, and OtbToolsProvider::loadAlgorithms registers every discovered otbcli_* binary this way. Most OTB applications do not use the '-in'/'-out' keys (e.g. TrainImagesClassifier uses io.il/io.out, BundleToPerfectSensor inp/inxs, ComputeImagesStatistics il/out.xml, BandMath il), so OTB's parser fails with 'Cannot find parameter -in'; buildArgs (generic_cli_algorithm.cpp:209-243) emits the template unconditionally and 'append_extra' EXTRA can only append arguments, never remove the invalid ones.`
- **PROVO-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `registry->addProvider(new GenericCliProvider) -> loadAlgorithms -> loadToolsFromDirectory(user dir) -> malformed user JSON -> QMessageBox::warning exec modal loop`
  - *Code context*: `'QMessageBox::warning(nullptr, QObject::tr("Tool Configuration Error"), ...)' on QJsonParseError and on missing 'id' - executed from loadAlgorithms(), which QgsProcessingRegistry calls during provider registration. In MCP/headless mode (no interactive user; parent nullptr) a modal static QMessageBox blocks the event loop indefinitely. Only user-writable ~/.sicnu_geo_rs/tools entries trigger it, but the same provider is constructed in headless executables.`
- **PROVO-8**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `GenericCliProvider::loadToolsFromDirectory -> otb_bilateral_filter tool in toolbox -> user picks 'bilateral' expecting edge preservation -> Gaussian smoothing destroys edges`
  - *Code context*: `otb_bilateral_filter.json: id 'otb_bilateral_filter', short_description/description 'OTB 双边滤波', help '边缘保持的平滑滤波' (edge-preserving smoothing = bilateral), tags ['bilateral'... via 'filter'] but args pass '-type gaussian -type.gaussian.stdev {SPATIAL}' to otbcli_Smoothing - a plain Gaussian blur, which is NOT edge-preserving; OTB Smoothing has no bilateral mode at all. Separately otb_median_filter.json still ships a mean filter (id otb_mean_filter); after #240 the id/name/args are self-consistent but the filename remains 'median' and no median tool exists.`
- **PROVO-9**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `OtbToolWrapper::processAlgorithm -> runOtbApplication succeeds (OTB wrote <path>.tif) -> QFileInfo::exists(<path>) false -> exception`
  - *Code context*: `'const QString outPath = resolvedParameters.value(param->name()).toString(); if (!outPath.isEmpty() && !QFileInfo::exists(outPath)) { ... "Tool reported success but output file is missing" ... throw QgsProcessingException(err); }' - OTB output parameters append a default extension (.tif for images, .shp for vectors) when the user-typed destination has none (the reason QGIS's own OTB provider post-executes a search for outputFile+'.tif'/'.shp'). The wrapper checks the literal typed path only, and inserts that same path into results, so a valid run with '/tmp/out' (OTB writes '/tmp/out.tif') is reported as failure and downstream consumers receive a nonexistent path.`
- **PROVO-10**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `otb_pixel_info run -> private QProcess loop -> nonzero exit -> readAllStandardError()==empty -> reportError without cause`
  - *Code context*: `'proc.setProcessChannelMode(QProcess::MergedChannels);' then on failure '.arg(QString::fromUtf8(proc.readAllStandardError()))' - with MergedChannels readAllStandardError() is always empty, so the message is 'failed with exit code N: ' with no diagnostic (the shared runOtbApplication in otb_tool_wrapper.cpp:208-211 correctly reads readAllStandardOutput). Both also skip the OTB_APPLICATION_PATH/PATH/LC_NUMERIC environment setup that runOtbApplication performs (otb_tool_wrapper.cpp:172-182), relying on otbcli self-location. Additionally feedback is dereferenced without a null check (lines 41,48 vs the shared runner's 'if (feedback)').`
- **PROVO-11**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `runOtbApplication -> QProcessEnvironment PATH rebuild with ':' -> proc.start on Windows -> launcher not found / dependency load failure`
  - *Code context*: `'const QString path = env.value(QStringLiteral("PATH")); env.insert(QStringLiteral("PATH"), binPath + (path.isEmpty() ? QString() : QStringLiteral(":") + path));' - on Windows the list separator is ';', so PATH becomes one invalid entry and otbcli plus its dependencies are not found from the bundle. The codebase otherwise targets Windows too (vcpkg manifest, QStandardPaths::findExecutable cross-platform comment in tool_path_manager.cpp:176-181). LD_LIBRARY_PATH handling in generic_cli_algorithm.cpp:363-365 is likewise Unix-only but harmless elsewhere.`
- **PROVO-12**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `qgis_algorithms:vector_distance_matrix with OUTPUT_TYPE=1 -> per-target rows appended linearly`
  - *Code context*: `Enum offers 'Linear (N * K x 4)' and 'Standard (N x M)' (lines 39-43); output fields are always InputID/TargetID/Distance(+Rank) (lines 101-109 with comment 'Linear output: always produces linear format'); in the writing loop 'if (outputType == 0) outputFeat.setAttribute("Rank", rank)' is the only place outputType is consulted (line 188). Selecting Standard yields the same linear table minus Rank - never an N x M matrix.`
- **PROVO-13**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `overlay run -> one combined symDiff geometry or per-intersection features written with unpopulated/missing attribute columns`
  - *Code context*: `vector_symmetrical_difference.cpp: sink is created with source->fields() (line 41) but the single emitted feature is default-constructed: 'QgsFeature outputFeat; outputFeat.setFields(source->fields()); outputFeat.setGeometry(symDiff);' - no setAttribute calls, so every record has empty attributes for the whole layer. native_intersection.h:57-58 creates the sink with 'source->fields()' only; makeIntersectionFeature copies input attributes only - QGIS native 'Intersection' concatenates overlay attributes (the shipped native_union.h:56-67 does build combined fields, showing the intended pattern).`
- **PROVO-14**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `qgis_algorithms:extractbyattribute -> per-feature string equality on attribute(fieldIdx)`
  - *Code context*: `'if (feat.attribute(fieldIdx).toString() == value) sink->addFeature(...)' - VALUE is a plain string, no OPERATOR parameter is defined (QGIS native extractbyattribute exposes =,!=,>,>=,<,<=,begins with, contains, is null/is not null). QVariant::toString() on numeric fields makes 5.0 vs typed '5.0'/'5' mismatches, doubles render with Qt formatting, and NULL attributes toString()=='' match an empty VALUE.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Highest-risk members verified. PROVO-7: GenericCliProvider::loadToolsFromDirectory pops a blocking QMessageBox::warning(nullptr, ...) on malformed tool JSON (provider.cpp:69-81) during provider registration — a malformed shipped manifest can hang headless/worker startup indefinitely; the shipped data/tools manifests currently parse, so it is trigger-latent but a real blocker when triggered. PROVO-13: vector_symmetrical_difference emits a single default-constructed feature (attributes dropped) (:119-126); native_intersection drops overlay attributes. Others (skeleton -in/-out auto-discovery, bilateral mislabel, extension-append check, merged stderr, hardcoded ':' PATH, distance-matrix type, extract-by-attr string compares) accepted from quoted evidence.
- **Review note**: None. P3 retained.
- **Finding PROVO-6**: `makeOtbDiscoveredConfig always inserts args ["-in","{INPUT}","-out","{OUTPUT}"] plus INPUT/OUTPUT parameter definitions, and OtbToolsProvider::loadAlgorithms registers every discovered otbcli_* binary this way. - **Finding PROVO-7**: `'QMessageBox::warning(nullptr, QObject::tr("Tool Configuration Error"), ...)' on QJsonParseError and on missing 'id' - executed from loadAlgorithms(), which QgsProcessingRegistry calls during provider registration. ## Suggested fix
- Align parameter definitions, argument builders, and result harvesting with the QGIS/GDAL/OTB provider execution contract.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (PROVO-6, PROVO-7, PROVO-8, PROVO-9, PROVO-10, PROVO-11, PROVO-12, PROVO-13, PROVO-14).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.
