======================================================================
ISSUE #354: [P3][error-handling] Classification minors batch: tile write/close errors ignored (disk-full => ok=true); sidecar validation always empty; predict-only band check counts-only; extraction rasterize without CRS check; post-process drops NoData=0; unbounded ROI extraction memory
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Classification minors batch: tile write/close errors ignored (disk-full => ok=true); sidecar validation always empty; predict-only band check counts-only; extraction rasterize without CRS check; post-process drops NoData=0; unbounded ROI extraction memory. This issue aggregates 6 related finding(s): RSCLS-5, RSCLS-6, RSCLS-7, RSCLS-8, RSCLS-9, RSCLS-10. Specifically, Pipeline ignores tile write and dataset-close errors — disk-full/IO failure produces result.ok=true with a truncated or zero-filled output. Specifically, Model sidecar's validation section is always empty — saveModelSidecar runs before the holdout accuracy it is documented to embed is computed. Specifically, Predict-only band-compatibility check compares only counts — a model trained on a same-size different band set is applied to misaligned features silently.

## Area
Error Handling / Exception Safety

## Type
error-handling

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/analysis/classification/rs_classification_pipeline.cpp:884-892 (unchecked GF_Write), 906-909 (unchecked GDALClose flush)` (RsClassificationPipeline::run tile loop)
- `src/analysis/classification/rs_classification_pipeline.cpp:382-410 (save with result.accuracy still default) vs 473-511 (accuracy computed afterwards)` (RsClassificationPipeline::run / RsClassificationPipeline::saveModelSidecar)
- `src/analysis/classification/rs_classification_pipeline.cpp:210-237` (RsClassificationPipeline::run (predict-only model check))
- `src/analysis/classification/rs_training_data_extraction.cpp:307-423 (extractFromVector -> collectPixels at 412 rasterizes raw OGR geometry via RsPixelRasterizer with the raster GT)` (RsTrainingDataExtraction::extractFromVector)
- `src/app/classification/rs_post_process_task.cpp:239-249 (saveLabelRaster called with quiet_NaN); rs_analysis loadLabelRaster (rs_post_process.cpp:399-456) never reads the source band NoData` (RsPostProcessTask::run / RsPostProcess::saveLabelRaster)
- `src/app/classification/qgsclassificationmainwindow.cpp:2212-2217 (Options with maxSamplesPerClass left 0 = unlimited; no UI control exists); src/analysis/classification/rs_training_data_extraction.cpp:82-130 (full X create)` (QgsClassificationMainWindow::buildTrainingData -> RsTrainingDataExtraction::extract/buildMatrices)

## User impact
- Silent corrupted output presented as a successful classification; downstream post-process/polygonize consumes the truncated map.
- Documented sidecar contract broken; model consumers (predict-only loads, RsClassifierLoadDialog) never see training-time validation numbers.
- Silently wrong predictions in a scenario the check explicitly claims to guard; compounded by unscaled models (scaler only checks width).
- Wrong or unusable training data on a CRS mismatch; #194's fix (0169bd7eac) touched 19 files, none in classification.
- Downstream contract violation consistent with the class of #202, but on the classification post-process path which that fix did not touch.
- Deterministic crash on plausible user actions (whole-region ROI); complexity O(ROIpixels x bands) memory with no bound.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (RSCLS-5)**: Output on a full or quota-exhausted volume (typical for compressed whole-scene class maps) mid-run.
- **Trigger (RSCLS-6)**: Train + save model with a holdout split (operator: modelOut + testSplit=0.3); inspect the .meta.json.
- **Trigger (RSCLS-7)**: User selects 3 bands on a new scene where the model was trained on a different 3-band combination (or reordered).
- **Trigger (RSCLS-8)**: Training polygons in EPSG:4326 over a UTM raster (or any CRS mismatch with geographic overlap): tiny/no pixels rasterize inside the raster extent.
- **Trigger (RSCLS-9)**: Run any post-process on a normal classified raster (which declares NoData=unclassified) and inspect the output's band NoData, or feed the output to a downstream mean/mask operator.
- **Trigger (RSCLS-10)**: 3000x3000-pixel rectangle ROI on a 224-band scene: 9e6 samples x 224 x 4B ~= 8.1 GB for X alone (plus ~150 MB index structures and 2 more copies after the split); even 1 MP x 224 bands ~= 0.9 GB before training starts.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (RSCLS-5)**: Write/close failure reported (Error::RasterWriteFailed) and partial output removed.
- **Expected (RSCLS-6)**: validation.overallAccuracy/kappa/perClass populated from the holdout run.
- **Expected (RSCLS-7)**: Reject when band identities differ from the sidecar features array (both are available).
- **Expected (RSCLS-8)**: CRS comparison + transform (or hard error) before rasterization, as done for the GUI path via RsRoiIO::load.
- **Expected (RSCLS-9)**: Source NoData=0 re-declared on the post-processed output (or an explicit unclassified policy).
- **Expected (RSCLS-10)**: A per-class cap (like the operator's maxSamplesPerClass=5000) or incremental/streaming extraction for the GUI path.

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (RSCLS-5)**: ok=true, 'Classification completed' logged, layer added to the map; unwritten tiles read back as NoData/0.
- **Actual (RSCLS-6)**: The 'validation' key is never emitted (accuracy.classIds empty at save time), so a later loadModelSidecar cannot restore validation metrics.
- **Actual (RSCLS-7)**: Prediction proceeds; feature columns fed to the model correspond to different spectral bands; garbage class map with ok=true.
- **Actual (RSCLS-8)**: Either a confusing 'No valid training pixels' failure or, when footprints partially overlap, a training set sampled from the wrong locations.
- **Actual (RSCLS-9)**: Output declares no NoData; 0/unclassified pixels are treated as class-0 data by downstream statistics, masks, and polygonize (background polygons).
- **Actual (RSCLS-10)**: Unbounded allocation on the GUI thread path; process aborts (OOM) or freezes the machine on hyperspectral scenes.

## Root cause
- **RSCLS-5**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `GUI Apply / operator -> pipeline tile loop -> band write; GTiff buffers tiles until cache flush/close, where ENOSPC surfaces as CE_Failure`
  - *Code context*: `dstDs->GetRasterBand( 1 )->RasterIO( GF_Write, dstX, dstY, tw, th, outBuf.data(), tw, th, GDT_Int32, 0, 0 ); — return value discarded (contrast the checked GF_Read at lines 746-754 and the checked write in RsPostProcess::saveLabelRaster:548).`
- **RSCLS-6**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Apply/operator with modelSavePath set and testSplit>0 -> model saved at 0.30 progress milestone, accuracy computed after`
  - *Code context*: `if ( !saveModelSidecar( config.modelSavePath, config.methodName, config.scaler, config.classColors, config.bandIndices, result.accuracy ) ) ... then later result.accuracy = RsAccuracyAssessment::compute( yt, yp ); — header lines 89-90 promise 'holdout validation metrics' in the sidecar.`
- **RSCLS-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `rs:supervised_classification modelIn with bands=[1,2,3] vs model trained on [3,2,1]/[2,4,6]`
  - *Code context*: `if ( !sidecarFeatures.isEmpty() && !config.bandIndices.isEmpty() && sidecarFeatures.size() != config.bandIndices.size() ) { ...error... } — the error text ('Select the same bands before applying the model') shows identity was intended, but element-wise equality is never tested; scaler band-count check also passes since sizes match.`
- **RSCLS-8**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `rs:supervised_classification(training=...) -> pipeline -> extractFromVector; GUI ROI shapefile path is safe (RsRoiIO::load transforms to target CRS) but the operator path is not`
  - *Code context*: `No QgsCoordinateReferenceSystem/OGRSpatialReference comparison anywhere in the file; geometries are consumed in whatever CRS the layer declares, directly in raster pixel space; the operator's only hint is the error string 'No valid training pixels extracted (check CRS overlap and classField)'.`
- **RSCLS-9**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `RsClassificationPipeline writes class map with NoData=0 (writeOutputNodata default true) -> GUI Sieve/Majority/Clump/Recode/merge-class dialog -> RsPostProcessTask -> output GTiff with no NoData value`
  - *Code context*: `// NaN → no GDAL NoData marker (caller preserves the source's semantics). if ( !RsPostProcess::saveLabelRaster( ..., std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN(), &err ) ) — but no caller preserves anything: loadLabelRaster returns only labels/gt/wkt, and saveLabelRaster skips SetNoDataValue when NaN (rs_post_process.cpp:573-574).`
- **RSCLS-10**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Rectangle/polygon ROI over a large area (magic wand is capped at 513x513, manual tools are not) -> buildTrainingData on Apply/Preview/CV`
  - *Code context*: `RsTrainingDataExtraction::Options options; options.ignore = currentIgnoreOptions(); options.minSamples = 10; — grep: the GUI never sets maxSamplesPerClass anywhere (operator defaults to 5000). buildMatrices: out.X.create( samples.size(), B, CV_32F ) plus a QPair sample list and byRow QHash over every pixel; stratifiedSplit then clones X into train+test.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Highest-risk members verified. RSCLS-5: rs_classification_pipeline.cpp:884-889 discards GF_Write return values, :906-909 GDALClose without checking flush error, then `result.ok = true` — disk-full/IO failure yields a truncated/zero-filled map presented as success (contrast with the checked GF_Read at :746-754 and RsPostProcess::saveLabelRaster:548). RSCLS-10: qgsclassificationmainwindow.cpp:2212-2217 builds RsTrainingDataExtraction::Options with maxSamplesPerClass left at its default 0 (unlimited) — no UI control sets it (operator default is 5000) — and buildMatrices does out.X.create(samples.size(), B) on every ROI pixel x band (:82-130); 3000x3000 x 224-band ~8.1GB X before training, deterministic OOM on plausible actions. RSCLS-9 verified (saveLabelRaster called with quiet_NaN so no GDAL NoData marker is set; unclassified 0 pixels become valid downstream).
- **Review note**: None. P3 retained.
- **Finding RSCLS-5**: `dstDs->GetRasterBand( 1 )->RasterIO( GF_Write, dstX, dstY, tw, th, outBuf.data(), tw, th, GDT_Int32, 0, 0 ); — return value discarded (contrast the checked GF_Read at lines 746-754 and the checked write in RsPostProcess::saveLabelRaster:548).`
- **Finding RSCLS-6**: `if ( !saveModelSidecar( config.modelSavePath, config.methodName, config.scaler, config.classColors, config.bandIndices, result.accuracy ) ) ... then later result.accuracy = RsAccuracyAssessment::compute( yt, yp ); — header lines 89-90 promise 'holdout validation metrics' in the sidecar.`
- **Finding RSCLS-7**: `if ( !sidecarFeatures.isEmpty() && !config.bandIndices.isEmpty() && sidecarFeatures.size() != config.bandIndices.size() ) { ...error... } — the error text ('Select the same bands before applying the model') shows identity was intended, but element-wise equality is never tested; scaler band-count check also passes since sizes match.`
- **Finding RSCLS-8**: `No QgsCoordinateReferenceSystem/OGRSpatialReference comparison anywhere in the file; geometries are consumed in whatever CRS the layer declares, directly in raster pixel space; the operator's only hint is the error string 'No valid training pixels extracted (check CRS overlap and classField)'.`
- **Finding RSCLS-9**: `// NaN → no GDAL NoData marker (caller preserves the source's semantics). if ( !RsPostProcess::saveLabelRaster( ..., std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN(), &err ) ) — but no caller preserves anything: loadLabelRaster returns only labels/gt/wkt, and saveLabelRaster skips SetNoDataValue when NaN (rs_post_process.cpp:573-574).`
- **Finding RSCLS-10**: `RsTrainingDataExtraction::Options options; options.ignore = currentIgnoreOptions(); options.minSamples = 10; — grep: the GUI never sets maxSamplesPerClass anywhere (operator defaults to 5000). buildMatrices: out.X.create( samples.size(), B, CV_32F ) plus a QPair sample list and byRow QHash over every pixel; stratifiedSplit then clones X into train+test.`

## Suggested fix
- Implement robust error reporting, return explicit error codes/exceptions, and prevent silent failures or unhandled exception aborts.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (RSCLS-5, RSCLS-6, RSCLS-7, RSCLS-8, RSCLS-9, RSCLS-10).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

## References / Dedupe
- RSCLS-10 touches #259 (per-tile discard) but is a distinct unbounded-memory defect; no prior issues for the others.
- Finding RSCLS-5: issues-closed.txt: #227 'partial outputs left behind' (opposite failure mode), #285 (pre-create deletion) — no closed issue covers unchecked write results in the pipeline.
- Finding RSCLS-6: grep issues-closed.txt for sidecar/validation: #242 (scaler missing, different); no closed issue on validation-in-sidecar ordering.
- Finding RSCLS-7: issues-closed.txt #242/#255/#186: none cover band-identity validation; check exists but is count-only.
- Finding RSCLS-8: #194 closed by 0169bd7eac; git show --stat verified no classification file touched; rs:supervised_classification unaffected at HEAD.
- Finding RSCLS-9: #202 closed by b3fe107887 covers SAM/RX/mosaic outputs; #190/#193 other operators; no closed issue covers rs_post_process_task NoData propagation.
- Finding RSCLS-10: issues-closed.txt: #201/#200 kmeans reservoir gate (operator path), #256/#214 GUI scan perf — none cover the uncapped GUI extraction memory.

ISSUE #356: [P3][ui/interaction] Shell/UI minors batch 3: Add-Layer menu registers empty path; swipe Space hijack; sample-data success count wrong; group-click clears current layer; offset-curve setting key mixup; hasM-from-hasZ; SchemaFormBuilder enum defaults; statistics null-deref; stubbed-editor degradations
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
Shell/UI minors batch 3: Add-Layer menu registers empty path; swipe Space hijack; sample-data success count wrong; group-click clears current layer; offset-curve setting key mixup; hasM-from-hasZ; SchemaFormBuilder enum defaults; statistics null-deref; stubbed-editor degradations. This issue aggregates 12 related finding(s): SHELLA-2, SHELLA-6, SHELLA-7, SHELLA-8, SHELLB-2, SHELLB-9, SHELLB-10, VPATCH-4, UIF-11, DLGA-10, DLGB-10, SHELLB-6. Specifically, Layer-tree context menu 'Add Raster/Vector Layer...' never opens a file dialog — it registers an empty path as a Missing asset and pops an 'asset not ready' error. Specifically, Swipe tool's Space-to-toggle-direction is hijacked by the global Space=Pan action shortcut — pressing Space silently replaces the swipe tool with pan; setMouseFollow(false) is also a no-op. Specifically, loadSampleData counts every file as loaded regardless of failures and reports 'Loaded N sample datasets' even when nothing loaded.

## Area
GUI / Application Shell / Dialogs

## Type
ui/interaction

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `src/app/layer_tree_menu.cpp:27-35, 92-100` (LayerTreeMenuProvider::createContextMenu)
- `src/app/map_tools/swipe_map_tool.cpp:155-176, 178-202; src/app/main_window_menus.cpp:288-289` (SwipeMapTool::keyPressEvent / SwipeMapTool::canvasMoveEvent / QgisDesktopWindow::setupMenu)
- `src/app/main_window_misc.cpp:174-207` (QgisDesktopWindow::loadSampleData)
- `src/app/main_window_connections.cpp:366-412` (QgisDesktopWindow::onLayerTreeClicked)
- `src/app/qgsmaptooloffsetcurve.cpp:736-740` (QgsOffsetUserWidget::QgsOffsetUserWidget)
- `src/app/qgsmaptooloffsetcurve.cpp:112-118 (line 113: `const bool hasM = QgsWkbTypes::hasZ( mSourceLayer->wkbType() );`)` (QgsMapToolOffsetCurve::cadCanvasReleaseEvent)
- `src/app/shell/schema_form_builder.cpp:339-376 (Enum field build: default applied only when `prop["default"].isString()`, 356-371); values() at 789-800 then submits combo currentData` (SchemaFormBuilder::buildField (FieldKind::Enum))
- `src/gui/vector/qgsvectorlayerproperties.cpp:456` (QgsVectorLayerProperties::QgsVectorLayerProperties)
- `src/ui/qgsprojecttrustdialog.ui:243 (also src/ui/qgsexpressionstoredialogbase.ui:51; src/ui/attributeformconfig/* and src/ui/qgsattributesforminitcode.ui:23)` (mScriptPreviewEditor / mExpression / (Attributes Form tab))
- `src/app/dialogs/comparison_dialog.cpp:106-136` (ComparisonDialog::loadLayerToWidget / ComparisonDialog::onLoadLayers)
- `src/app/dialogs/stac_browser_dialog.cpp:156-185` (StacBrowserDialog::loadSelectedAsset)
- `src/app/qgsmaptoolrotatefeature.cpp:353-365 (cancel, deref at 358); 465-473 (keyReleaseEvent Escape path with no layer check); same pattern in src/app/qgsmaptoolscalefeature.cpp:306-318 (deref at 311)` (QgsMapToolRotateFeature::cancel / QgsMapToolScaleFeature::cancel)

## User impact
- Both context-menu entries are visible-but-broken (same class as #214's no-op); users get a confusing error and a phantom catalog entry per click
- Swipe keyboard affordance is dead and destructive (silently cancels the comparison); minor API dead switch
- Misleading success feedback masks broken sample datasets in the primary demo entry point
- Frequent accidental de-selection in normal legend browsing; every raster tool that keys off activeLayer then bounces the user to re-select the layer
- User preference loss + silent corruption of a digitizing conversion parameter (over-aggressive or disabled curve conversion)
- Misleading user warning only; likely inherited from upstream QGIS but divergent from the in-tree chamfer/fillet sibling
- Silent wrong-parameter submissions for affected operators; violates the schema-defaults contract
- Crash on an edge-but-real path; cheap fix.
- Degraded but non-destructive UI in three low-traffic dialogs (the Attributes Form tab is a whole missing feature surface, still non-destructive).
- UI freeze proportional to provider latency; nested event loop during wait allows reentrant input (double 加载 clicks start overlapping jobs); inconsistent with the codebase's TaskCenter/async pattern used by processing dialogs
- Unresponsive UI during STAC import; repeated clicks possible during the freeze
- Deterministic crash of the app in a layer-removal-mid-edit scenario — matches the audit focus on layer-removal crash paths

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (SHELLA-2)**: Right-click anywhere in the 视图图层 layer tree and pick 'Add Raster Layer...' or 'Add Vector Layer...'
- **Trigger (SHELLA-6)**: Activate 卷帘对比, then press Space (documented direction toggle), or call setMouseFollow(false) and move the mouse
- **Trigger (SHELLA-7)**: Help -> Load Sample Data with any unreadable/moved file in data/samples (or on a build whose sample dir has non-loadable files)
- **Trigger (SHELLA-8)**: With a raster layer selected, expand its legend in 视图图层 and click a legend class row or any group header
- **Trigger (SHELLB-2)**: Use the Offset Curve map tool and click its advanced visibility toggle
- **Trigger (SHELLB-9)**: Use Offset Curve on a PolygonZ (no M) or PointM layer
- **Trigger (SHELLB-10)**: Open a task-panel tool whose schema has a numeric enum with a non-zero default (e.g. default index/value != first item)
- **Trigger (VPATCH-4)**: Open properties of a broken vector layer (e.g., datasource deleted after add, or unknown provider).
- **Trigger (UIF-11)**: Open each dialog in the listed workflow.
- **Trigger (DLGA-10)**: Open 图层对比 from the change-detection dialog (or click 加载) with large/remote raster layers
- **Trigger (DLGB-10)**: Select a STAC item whose COG lives on a slow/unresponsive host and click 加载选中资产
- **Trigger (SHELLB-6)**: Remove the active vector layer while the rotate/scale rubber band is active, then press Escape

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (SHELLA-2)**: A file-open dialog appears and the chosen file is loaded (matching the 图层 menu Add Raster Layer... behavior)
- **Expected (SHELLA-6)**: Space toggles vertical/horizontal swipe direction (and a disabled mouse-follow freezes the divider)
- **Expected (SHELLA-7)**: Count only successful loads and report failures (e.g. '成功 X，失败 Y' like addRasterLayer at main_window_layers.cpp:205-208)
- **Expected (SHELLA-8)**: Legend/group clicks leave the active layer untouched (QGIS keeps currentLayer when interacting with legend nodes); only explicit layer selection changes it
- **Expected (SHELLB-2)**: settingsDigitizingOffsetShowAdvanced persists the toggle; curve tolerance untouched
- **Expected (SHELLB-9)**: hasM reflects QgsWkbTypes::hasM
- **Expected (SHELLB-10)**: Combo preselects the schema default
- **Expected (VPATCH-4)**: Null-check dataProvider() as the neighboring upstream code does.
- **Expected (UIF-11)**: Script preview text; expression shown; functional form-config tab.
- **Expected (DLGA-10)**: Preview render dispatched asynchronously (or rendered at reduced detail) so the dialog opens immediately
- **Expected (DLGB-10)**: Remote layer open deferred to a worker (task/QtConcurrent) with progress feedback
- **Expected (SHELLB-6)**: cancel() null-checks (as the mouse path effectively does)

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (SHELLA-2)**: No dialog; a modal warning box 'Open Raster — [display.asset_not_ready] The Data Asset is not ready for display' appears; a junk Missing asset is also registered into the Data Manager catalog and fires an autoDisplayFailed log
- **Actual (SHELLA-6)**: Space switches the map tool to pan and kills the active swipe session; mouse-follow disable is ignored (divider keeps tracking)
- **Actual (SHELLA-7)**: Status bar reports the total file count as loaded even if every load failed
- **Actual (SHELLA-8)**: Active layer is silently cleared and dependent chrome (band rail, opacity, layer status, active-raster dialogs) resets
- **Actual (SHELLB-2)**: Preference not persisted; unrelated numeric setting overwritten with a boolean
- **Actual (SHELLB-9)**: hasM mirrors hasZ; message wrong in both directions
- **Actual (SHELLB-10)**: Combo sits on the first enum item; run parameters drift from operator defaults
- **Actual (VPATCH-4)**: Unconditional dereference — nullptr crash.
- **Actual (UIF-11)**: Blank preview; blank expression display; empty Attributes Form page.
- **Actual (DLGA-10)**: GUI blocks inside waitForFinished per layer; two sequential blocking renders before the dialog is even visible
- **Actual (DLGB-10)**: GUI freezes for the duration of the vsicurl open (seconds to tens of seconds on degraded networks)
- **Actual (SHELLB-6)**: Null pointer dereference crash

## Root cause
- **SHELLA-2**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `right-click layer tree (empty area or any layer node) -> LayerTreeMenuProvider::createContextMenu -> 'Add Raster Layer...' -> ActiveViewHost::openRasterPath("") -> DataManager::registerSource("",Missing) -> QgisDisplayManager::addLayer -> 'display.asset_not_ready' failure -> QMessageBox warning`
  - *Code context*: `layer_tree_menu.cpp:29-31 `menu->addAction(QObject::tr("Add Raster Layer..."), menu, [hostPtr]() { if (hostPtr) hostPtr->openRasterPath(QString()); });` — passes an EMPTY path to ActiveViewHost::openRasterPath instead of opening QFileDialog (the main-menu equivalent QgisDesktopWindow::addRasterLayer does open a dialog). Chain: openRasterPath("") -> openSource -> DataManager::registerSource -> GdalRasterSourceProvider::resolve returns state=Missing (gdal_raster_source_provider.cpp:131-137 `if (!info.exists()...) { resolved.state = AssetState::Missing; ... return success; }`) -> asset registers and emits assetAdded -> QgisDisplayManager::addLayer rejects with 'display.asset_not_ready' -> openSource returns failure -> reportDiagnostics -> QMessageBox::warning. Same for the vector entry and for the duplicates appended to every node context menu at lines 93-99.`
- **SHELLA-6**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `toggleSwipeTool (Ctrl+Shift+S) -> SwipeMapTool active -> user presses Space -> QAction('平移', QKeySequence('Space')) shortcut wins over QgsMapCanvas keyPressEvent -> QgisDesktopWindow::panMap -> swipe tool deactivated`
  - *Code context*: `swipe_map_tool.cpp:180-183 toggles direction on Qt::Key_Space; but main_window_menus.cpp:288-289 registers `viewMenu->addAction( ic("p_n"), tr("平移"), QKeySequence("Space"), this, &QgisDesktopWindow::panMap )` on the (hidden but shortcut-active) detached menubar with default Qt::WindowShortcut context. QAction shortcuts are matched before key events reach QgsMapCanvas/the map tool, so Space always triggers panMap -> setMapTool(m_panTool), deactivating the swipe tool instead of toggling direction — the same ambiguity class as #212 (Ctrl+V Paste vs Vertex Tool). Additionally canvasMoveEvent (:155-165) never consults m_mouseFollow although the header documents 'when disabled it stays at the current position' — setMouseFollow(false) has no effect.`
- **SHELLA-7**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `帮助->加载示例数据 -> loadRasterLayer/loadVectorLayer per file (return ignored) -> statusBar 'Loaded N sample datasets'`
  - *Code context*: `main_window_misc.cpp:192-195 `for (const QFileInfo &file : files) { loadRasterLayer(file.absoluteFilePath()); loaded++; }` — the bool return of loadRasterLayer -> ActiveViewHost::openRasterPath is discarded and loaded++ runs unconditionally (same for shapefiles at :201-204). A corrupt/unsupported sample file yields an error dialog per file plus a final status line claiming 'Loaded N sample datasets' for all N files.`
- **SHELLA-8**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `layer tree click on legend/group row -> onLayerTreeClicked else-branch -> setCurrentLayer(nullptr) -> QgsMapCanvas::currentLayerChanged -> syncActiveLayerChrome -> band rail cleared / syncStatusBarLayer(无图层)`
  - *Code context*: `main_window_connections.cpp:407-411: `} else { m_mapCanvas->setCurrentLayer(nullptr); updateEditingUI(nullptr); syncStatusBarLayer(nullptr); }` — the else branch fires whenever the clicked index is not a layer node. initLayerTree (active_view_host.cpp:157-158) enables ShowLegend + ShowLegendAsTree, so raster legend class rows are real clickable rows; clicking one (e.g. to inspect/toggle a legend class) or a group header wipes currentLayer. Consequences: m_bandRail->setRasterLayer(nullptr) via the layersChanged/currentLayerChanged chrome sync (main_window_connections.cpp:62-74), status shows 无图层, opacity slider disabled, and findActiveRaster-based dialogs (ROI 均值谱, Contrast Stretch, …) then refuse to run until the user re-clicks the layer name row.`
- **SHELLB-2**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Activate Offset Curve tool -> click the advanced-options toggle (any state) -> settingsDigitizingConvertToCurveDistanceTolerance := 0.0 or 1.0; later digitizing with curve conversion enabled uses the corrupted tolerance; reopening the offset tool always shows the default advanced state`
  - *Code context*: ``const bool showAdvanced = QgsSettingsRegistryCore::settingsDigitizingOffsetShowAdvanced->value();` (line 736) restores the state, but the toggle handler writes a different key: `connect( mShowAdvancedButton, &QToolButton::clicked, this, []( const bool clicked ) { QgsSettingsRegistryCore::settingsDigitizingConvertToCurveDistanceTolerance->setValue( clicked ); } );` (line 740). Upstream QGIS writes settingsDigitizingOffsetShowAdvanced here. Two effects: (1) the advanced-panel visibility preference is read but never written, so it never persists; (2) every click stores 0.0/1.0 into the convert-to-curve distance tolerance, which is a live digitizing parameter consumed by `src/gui/maptools/qgsmaptoolcapture.cpp:390` (`linear.convertToCurves( settingsDigitizingConvertToCurveDistanceTolerance->value(), ...angleTolerance )`) — corrupting the user's configured curve-conversion tolerance to 0 or 1 after any offset-tool session.`
- **SHELLB-9**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Offset tool first click on a feature -> warning construction`
  - *Code context*: ``const bool hasZ = QgsWkbTypes::hasZ( mSourceLayer->wkbType() ); const bool hasM = QgsWkbTypes::hasZ( mSourceLayer->wkbType() );` — the second line should call QgsWkbTypes::hasM. The sibling chamfer/fillet tool implements both correctly (qgsmaptoolchamferfillet.cpp:381-382 uses hasZ then hasM), so this is a copy-paste divergence between sibling tools. Impact is confined to the warning message text ("layer X has Z/M geometry. Z/M values be set to 0...") — geometry handling itself does not depend on the flag.`
- **SHELLB-10**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `openTool -> panel->showTool(schema) -> SchemaFormBuilder::rebuild -> buildField(Enum) -> default skipped -> user hits Run -> formValues()/values() submits combo item 0`
  - *Code context*: ``if ( prop.isMember( "default" ) ) { if ( prop["default"].isString() ) { ...findData/setCurrentIndex... } }` — a JSON-Schema enum like {"enum":[1,2,3],"default":2} adds numeric combo data (line 350-351: `combo->addItem( QString::number( v.asDouble() ), v.asDouble() )`) but the default branch handles strings only, so the combo stays at index 0. writeFieldValue() (723-741) does handle numeric enum values, so only the initial default is lost. Every operator whose schema declares numeric enum defaults silently gets item 0 from the task panel — a form-vs-schema defaults drift, exactly the FOCUS category; string enums are unaffected.`
- **VPATCH-4**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Layer properties on a vector layer whose provider failed to construct (dataProvider() == nullptr, layer invalid but present in project/layer tree) -> ActiveViewHost::showLayerProperties has no validity gate -> ctor dereferences null.`
  - *Code context*: `Delta line: 'const bool hasSpatialIndex = mLayer->dataProvider()->capabilities() & Qgis::VectorProviderCapability::CreateSpatialIndex;' — while the surrounding upstream code null-guards the provider ('const QgsVectorDataProvider *provider = mLayer->dataProvider();' followed by checks).`
- **UIF-11**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `Respective dialogs: open untrusted project with embedded scripts; save stored expression; vector layer properties -> Attributes Form tab.`
  - *Code context*: `1) qgsprojecttrustdialog.ui:243 QgsCodeEditorPython mScriptPreviewEditor - consumer qgsprojecttrustdialog.cpp:55 setText no-op -> embedded-project macro script preview always blank (readonly, cosmetic). 2) qgsexpressionstoredialogbase.ui:51 QgsCodeEditorExpression mExpression - qgsexpressionstoredialog.cpp:32 setText no-op -> dialog displays the expression being saved as empty (label/help still saved). 3) Vector Layer Properties Attributes Form tab (mOptsPage_AttributesForm, qgsvectorlayerpropertiesbase.ui:896-911; widget created qgsvectorlayerproperties.cpp:237): QgsAttributesFormProperties is replaced by the stub in antigravity_codeeditor_stubs.cpp whose ctor never calls setupUi and whose init()/apply() are empty - the tab renders a blank page and drag-and-drop form configuration silently does nothing. The attributeformconfig/*.ui forms and qgsattributesforminitcode.ui are consequently dead-loaded.`
- **DLGA-10**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `双视图对比… click → openComparisonPreview → setLeftLayer/setRightLayer → loadLayerToWidget ×2 → job.start(); job.waitForFinished() (GUI thread blocked in nested loop) → dialog finally shown; same again on each 加载 click`
  - *Code context*: `Lines 128-136: `QgsMapSettings mapSettings; ... QgsMapRendererParallelJob job(mapSettings); job.start(); job.waitForFinished(); const QImage image = job.renderedImage();` — a blocking nested wait in a click slot. onLoadLayers calls it for both layers back-to-back (lines 106-110), and ChangeDetectionDialog::openComparisonPreview (change_detection_dialog.cpp:260-276) calls setLeftLayer+setRightLayer before exec(), so opening the preview blocks on two full renders before the dialog appears. For slow providers (remote/WMS sources, uncached COG over network) the wait lasts the full provider timeout.`
- **DLGB-10**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `加载选中资产 click -> loadSelectedAsset -> QgisDesktopWindow::loadDataLayer -> ActiveViewHost::openPath -> QgsRasterLayer(/vsicurl/...) sync network open -> GUI blocked -> zoomToFullExtent -> accept()`
  - *Code context*: `'if ( !mw->loadDataLayer( vsicurl ) )' — loadDataLayer (main_window_layers.cpp:315-320) synchronously calls m_activeViewHost->openPath(filePath), which constructs a QgsRasterLayer on the '/vsicurl/https://...' URL selected by StacClient::selectCogHref. QgsRasterLayer construction performs the GDAL vsicurl header fetch (network round trips, retries on slow hosts) inline on the calling (GUI) thread; only the search itself is async via StacClient.`
- **SHELLB-6**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `start rotation (click feature) -> layer deleted (layer panel remove / project close while tool active) -> press Escape -> keyReleaseEvent -> cancel -> vlayer->selectedFeatureCount() on nullptr`
  - *Code context*: ``void QgsMapToolRotateFeature::cancel() { deleteRotationWidget(); deleteRubberband(); QgsVectorLayer *vlayer = currentVectorLayer(); if ( vlayer->selectedFeatureCount() == 0 || mAutoSetAnchorPoint ) ...` — vlayer is dereferenced unconditionally. The right-click path is safe because cadCanvasReleaseEvent checks `!vlayer` first (lines 197-206) and returns before calling cancel, but keyReleaseEvent (Escape while mRotationActive) calls cancel() directly with no check. If the edited layer was removed from the project while a rotation drag is active (mRotationActive==true, mRubberBand set), currentVectorLayer() returns nullptr -> nullptr deref -> SIGSEGV. Identical code in scalefeature cancel(). (Inherited from upstream QGIS, but a real crash path in this tree.)`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Crash member verified. SHELLB-6: qgsmaptoolrotatefeature.cpp:353-365 cancel() dereferences currentVectorLayer() unconditionally (:358) and keyReleaseEvent Escape path (:465-473) calls cancel() with no layer check (the right-click path :197-206 checks !vlayer first); removing the edited layer mid-rotation then Escape => nullptr deref => SIGSEGV. Idential pattern in qgsmaptoolscalefeature.cpp:306-318. UIF-11 verified as real-but-cosmetic stubbed editors (qgsprojecttrustdialog.cpp:55 setText no-op; expression store dialog blank; attributes-form init tab empty).
- **Review note**: SHELLB-6 is the batch's only crash member and is the strongest justification for keeping the batch; UIF-11 is a cosmetic/degradation item.

Two effects: (1) the advanced-panel visibility preference is read but never written, so it never persists; (2) every click stores 0.0/1.0 into the convert-to-curve distance tolerance, which is a live digitizing parameter consumed by `src/gui/maptools/qgsmaptoolcapture.cpp:390` (`linear.convertToCurves( settingsDigitizingConvertToCurveDistanceTolerance->value(), ...angleTolerance )`) — corrupting the user's configured curve-conversion tolerance to 0 or 1 after any offset-tool session.`
- **Finding SHELLB-9**: ``const bool hasZ = QgsWkbTypes::hasZ( mSourceLayer->wkbType() ); const bool hasM = QgsWkbTypes::hasZ( mSourceLayer->wkbType() );` — the second line should call QgsWkbTypes::hasM. The sibling chamfer/fillet tool implements both correctly (qgsmaptoolchamferfillet.cpp:381-382 uses hasZ then hasM), so this is a copy-paste divergence between sibling tools. Impact is confined to the warning message text ("layer X has Z/M geometry. Z/M values be set to 0...") — geometry handling itself does not depend on the flag.`
- **Finding SHELLB-10**: ``if ( prop.isMember( "default" ) ) { if ( prop["default"].isString() ) { ...findData/setCurrentIndex... } }` — a JSON-Schema enum like {"enum":[1,2,3],"default":2} adds numeric combo data (line 350-351: `combo->addItem( QString::number( v.asDouble() ), v.asDouble() )`) but the default branch handles strings only, so the combo stays at index 0. writeFieldValue() (723-741) does handle numeric enum values, so only the initial default is lost. Every operator whose schema declares numeric enum defaults silently gets item 0 from the task panel — a form-vs-schema defaults drift, exactly the FOCUS category; string enums are unaffected.`
- **Finding VPATCH-4**: `Delta line: 'const bool hasSpatialIndex = mLayer->dataProvider()->capabilities() & Qgis::VectorProviderCapability::CreateSpatialIndex;' — while the surrounding upstream code null-guards the provider ('const QgsVectorDataProvider *provider = mLayer->dataProvider();' followed by checks).`
- **Finding UIF-11**: `1) qgsprojecttrustdialog.ui:243 QgsCodeEditorPython mScriptPreviewEditor - consumer qgsprojecttrustdialog.cpp:55 setText no-op -> embedded-project macro script preview always blank (readonly, cosmetic). 2) qgsexpressionstoredialogbase.ui:51 QgsCodeEditorExpression mExpression - qgsexpressionstoredialog.cpp:32 setText no-op -> dialog displays the expression being saved as empty (label/help still saved). 3) Vector Layer Properties Attributes Form tab (mOptsPage_AttributesForm, qgsvectorlayerpropertiesbase.ui:896-911; widget created qgsvectorlayerproperties.cpp:237): QgsAttributesFormProperties is replaced by the stub in antigravity_codeeditor_stubs.cpp whose ctor never calls setupUi and whose init()/apply() are empty - the tab renders a blank page and drag-and-drop form configuration silently does nothing. The attributeformconfig/*.ui forms and qgsattributesforminitcode.ui are consequently dead-loaded.`
- **Finding DLGA-10**: `Lines 128-136: `QgsMapSettings mapSettings; ... QgsMapRendererParallelJob job(mapSettings); job.start(); job.waitForFinished(); const QImage image = job.renderedImage();` — a blocking nested wait in a click slot. onLoadLayers calls it for both layers back-to-back (lines 106-110), and ChangeDetectionDialog::openComparisonPreview (change_detection_dialog.cpp:260-276) calls setLeftLayer+setRightLayer before exec(), so opening the preview blocks on two full renders before the dialog appears. For slow providers (remote/WMS sources, uncached COG over network) the wait lasts the full provider timeout.`
- **Finding DLGB-10**: `'if ( !mw->loadDataLayer( vsicurl ) )' — loadDataLayer (main_window_layers.cpp:315-320) synchronously calls m_activeViewHost->openPath(filePath), which constructs a QgsRasterLayer on the '/vsicurl/https://...' URL selected by StacClient::selectCogHref. QgsRasterLayer construction performs the GDAL vsicurl header fetch (network round trips, retries on slow hosts) inline on the calling (GUI) thread; only the search itself is async via StacClient.`
- **Finding SHELLB-6**: ``void QgsMapToolRotateFeature::cancel() { deleteRotationWidget(); deleteRubberband(); QgsVectorLayer *vlayer = currentVectorLayer(); if ( vlayer->selectedFeatureCount() == 0 || mAutoSetAnchorPoint ) ...` — vlayer is dereferenced unconditionally. The right-click path is safe because cadCanvasReleaseEvent checks `!vlayer` first (lines 197-206) and returns before calling cancel, but keyReleaseEvent (Escape while mRotationActive) calls cancel() directly with no check. If the edited layer was removed from the project while a rotation drag is active (mRotationActive==true, mRubberBand set), currentVectorLayer() returns nullptr -> nullptr deref -> SIGSEGV. Identical code in scalefeature cancel(). (Inherited from upstream QGIS, but a real crash path in this tree.)`

## Suggested fix
- Ensure widgets are fully initialized or stubbed safely, signals/slots are correctly connected to backend results, and UI elements do not block the main event loop.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (SHELLA-2, SHELLA-6, SHELLA-7, SHELLA-8, SHELLB-2, SHELLB-9, SHELLB-10, VPATCH-4, UIF-11, DLGA-10, DLGB-10, SHELLB-6).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
- Low risk when applying surgical, isolated fixes matching existing conventions.

======================================================================
ISSUE #358: [P3][tests/contract] gdal test suites exercise zero first-party code while real wrapper defects ship (coverage illusion)
Labels: ['bug']
Body:
## Summary
gdal test suites exercise zero first-party code while real wrapper defects ship (coverage illusion). Specifically, Platform-behavior tests masquerade as product coverage in the gdal tool/error suites — zero first-party code exercised while real wrapper defects were shipping.

## Area
Test Suite / Test Contracts & Invariants

## Type
tests/contract

## Severity
P3

## Baseline
- Repository: WindWang2/exp-rs
- Branch: master
- Commit: d9c59e5b8290a74cbfe6c8c9f3c7209c6cdab7dc

## Affected code
- `tests/test_gdal_tool_wrapper.cpp:9-86 (also tests/test_gdal_null_band.cpp:19-104, tests/test_gdal_safe_call.cpp:49-70, tests/test_gdal_wrapper.cpp:245-256)` (TEST_CASE("QProcess MergedChannels captures stderr") et al.)

## User impact
- False coverage signal on the CLI wrapper error-handling seam; combined with TESTS-1's tautological bandNoDataValue test, the GDAL error/nodata contract has effectively no behavioral pinning.

## Reproduction
- **Trigger (TESTS-4)**: Any change to first-party gdal tool wrapper / error capture code: these suites cannot fail.

## Expected behavior
- **Expected (TESTS-4)**: Tests execute the first-party wrapper (e.g. gdal_tools executor with a failing CLI) and assert captured stderr/error JSON.

## Actual behavior
- **Actual (TESTS-4)**: 4 passing test cases that assert Qt/QProcess and GDAL library behavior only.

## Root cause
- **TESTS-4**: confirmed
  - *Callchain*: `ctest reports 'Gdal tool wrapper error handling' tests as passing -> reviewers skip deepening coverage -> first-party wrapper error paths regress undetected`
  - *Code context*: `test_gdal_tool_wrapper.cpp is titled 'Test GDAL tool wrapper error handling' and its 4 cases spin QProcess('bash') to assert Qt's MergedChannels/SeparateChannels semantics — the file never includes, links, or calls any first-party wrapper (no gdal_tools symbols; CMake target links only Qt6::Core + Catch2). test_gdal_null_band.cpp's 4 cases call raw GDALCreate/GDALGetRasterBand/GDALRasterIO and assert GDAL's own null-band behavior; line 81 admits it: 'This test demonstrates the pattern the dialog code should use'. test_gdal_safe_call.cpp:49-70 'Safe open with error handling' hand-writes a try/catch around GDALOpen instead of exercising GDAL_SAFE_CALL on a first-party path. test_gdal_wrapper.cpp:245-256 covers bandNoDataValue with (void)nodata; (void)hasNodata; REQUIRE(true). These create an appearance of coverage over exactly the seams where real defects later surfaced from other audits: gdal CLI wrappers dropped FileDestination outputs and emitted wrong CLI arguments (#196/#169) and swallow failures (#228). The tests are all green while testing none of that code.`

## Evidence
- **Skeptic verification**: Verified: test_gdal_tool_wrapper.cpp:9-86 fires QProcess('bash') and asserts Qt's MergedChannels/SeparateChannels semantics — the title says 'GDAL tool wrapper error handling' but no first-party symbol appears (no include, no link of wrappers). Sibling files (test_gdal_null_band.cpp, test_gdal_safe_call.cpp:49-70, test_gdal_wrapper.cpp:245-256) share the pattern per evidence. This is a naming/coverage illusion finding rather than a product bug — valid as a P3 test-hygiene item.
- **Review note**: Classify as test-hygiene (coverage metric abusoire), not a product defect; severity P3 stands.
- **Finding TESTS-4**: `test_gdal_tool_wrapper.cpp is titled 'Test GDAL tool wrapper error handling' and its 4 cases spin QProcess('bash') to assert Qt's MergedChannels/SeparateChannels semantics — the file never includes, links, or calls any first-party wrapper (no gdal_tools symbols; CMake target links only Qt6::Core + Catch2). test_gdal_null_band.cpp's 4 cases call raw GDALCreate/GDALGetRasterBand/GDALRasterIO and assert GDAL's own null-band behavior; line 81 admits it: 'This test demonstrates the pattern the dialog code should use'. test_gdal_safe_call.cpp:49-70 'Safe open with error handling' hand-writes a try/catch around GDALOpen instead of exercising GDAL_SAFE_CALL on a first-party path. test_gdal_wrapper.cpp:245-256 covers bandNoDataValue with (void)nodata; (void)hasNodata; REQUIRE(true). These create an appearance of coverage over exactly the seams where real defects later surfaced from other audits: gdal CLI wrappers dropped FileDestination outputs and emitted wrong CLI arguments (#196/#169) and swallow failures (#228). The tests are all green while testing none of that code.`

## Suggested fix
- Address the root cause directly with minimal, surgical changes adhering to project coding standards.

## Acceptance criteria
- Defect resolved for all members (TESTS-4).
- CMake build and Catch2 unit test suite pass with 100% success.
- No regression in existing functionality or contracts.

## Regression tests
- Add targeted unit/integration tests covering the affected code path(s).

## Risk / compatibility
